she crystallizes as Demi-Lich in a phylactery\n    13→- [[Tygras]] makes a deal with an arch-fey from [[Vecna]]'s plane; creates a weapon to bridge worlds\n    14→- [[Ranar Surefoot]] steals the [[Diadem of Dawn]] during the weapon's use; the weapon backfires\n    15→- [[Tygras]]'s superweapon destroys [[uma]], creating the desert; [[Tygras]] sealed in [[Harvest's Zenith]]\n    16→- The [[Matrim Pact]] formed: human sailors, gnomes of [[Fae'ish]], and [[Qual'Xar]] half-elves\n    17→- Covenants of Magic established after [[Tygras]]'s destruction — no being except the Alabaster Seat may possess such power again\n    18→- [[Kolyma]] (Last Curator) establishes Zodiac guardianship of the Zeniths\n    19→- The [[forgesworn]] order established; [[Thorin the Bronze|Thorin Bronzebeard]] builds the original Warforged army\n    20→\n    21→## Recent Past (Decades Before Campaign)\n    22→\n    23→- ~26 years ago: [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] born; parents make a deal with [[Reynardine]] for the philosopher's stone\n    24→- ~8 years ago: Nyx's dominant soul emerges; her sister taken; parents spurn Nyx\n    25→- ~7 years ago: [[Xyn]] born — the \"perfect vessel\" for the arch-fey's plan\n    26→- [[Mordred]] possesses [[Finkle Hollingsworth|Finkle Hollingsworth]] (Gnome, NOT Goblin) at [[Forest's Zenith]]\n    27→- [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] founded as a soul-harvesting front\n    28→- [[Thorkon Fireforge]] betrays [[Thorson Bronzebeard II]] at [[Ghost-Eye Thorrin]]'s urging\n    29→- [[Ghost-Eye Thorrin]] betrays [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]], hunts all [[forgesworn]]\n    30→- [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]] and [[Hlin Blunderbeat|Hlin]] flee [[Vag'Mahar]]; [[Hlin Blunderbeat|Hlin]] dies giving birth to [[Daina Fireforge|Daina]] and an older brother\n    31→- [[Blood Star]] kills [[Thorson Bronzebeard II]]\n    32→- [[Oblivion Wayne]] deposes her brother, unites all Drow under vampire rule\n    33→- [[Ebon Wayne]] captures [[Zodiac|Bat Zodiac]], discovers [[Spell Plague]]\n    34→- [[Vecna]] raises [[Peraxis Phyrexia|Peraxis Geth]] as Illithilich\n    35→- [[Peraxis Phyrexia]] corrupts the Elder Brain\n    36→- [[Ned'Zailok]] attacks [[Tower Bridge]]; [[Varis Liadon|Varis]]'s father [[Yeldan Liadon|Yeldan]] goes missing\n    37→- [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] covers the evacuation; [[Varis Liadon|Varis]] enlists\n    38→- [[Ned'Zailok]] defeated after 18 months of fighting\n    39→\n    40→## Campaign Era (136 Chapters)\n    41→\n    42→### Act 1 (Chapters 0-13) — The Awakening\n    43→\n    44→| Ch. | Event |\n    45→|-----|-------|\n    46→| 0 | Party recruited into [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]]. [[Jorgen]] arrives pursued by kobolds. [[Sabine Ra]] freed from phylactery, sacrifices herself. Crystalline hearts deposited in party. |\n    49→| 3-5 | [[Artificer's Guild]] rescue. [[Xerxes]] exposition. [[jafar]]/Sally intrigue. |\n    52→| 9 | First Prophecy ([[Ruth]]). |\n    53→| 11 | [[Golden Reach]] demons (Danelle/Markus) exposed at Festival of Light. [[Stalwart Little Goldentooth|Stalwart]]'s divine sense. |\n    54→| 12 | Second Prophecy (Hags). Pestilence discovered — brewed from children's souls. |\n    55→| 13 | Third Prophecy ([[Mia]]). [[Mia]] reveals she wiped party's memories. |\n    56→\n    57→### Act 2 (Chapters 14-40) — The Voyage\n    58→\n    59→| Ch. | Event |\n    60→|-----|-------|\n    61→| 17 | [[Nathan Yanagan]] (spy for [[jafar]]) aboard [[Thornberry Shade]]. Four Zenith hieroglyphics. |\n    65→| 26 | [[Kelik Waystar]] revealed as air genasi prince. |\n    66→| 29 | Green Tides of St. Ashbury warning. |\n    67→| 30-31 | [[Peraxis Phyrexia]] revealed. [[Peraxis Phyrexia|Peraxis]] reveals complex motivations — wanted to save the party. |\n    88→| 107 | Ragnarok. Warforged awakening. [[Ghost-Eye Thorrin]] disappears. |\n    91→| 113 | [[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]] framed for Pestilence \"oil spill.\" [[rimefang]]'s last ride. |\n    92→| 116 | Convergence and Betrayal — all planes converging into the Feywild. |\n    93→| 119 | Time paradox — [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] finds own dead body from 2 weeks ago, kickstarts his own heart. |\n    94→| 133 | [[Reynaldine]]'s true plan revealed: orchestrated everything to ascend to godhood and save Gabriel. [[Tiamat]] at Harvest Zenith. |\n    96→| 136 | [[Asmodeus]] killed by [[Josh Coolbreeze]]. Three specters: one into [[Nocturnus Wayne|Nocturnus]]. [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] becomes deity of Mardov's Feywild via Stone Table sacrifice. |\n    97→\n    98→## Prophesied: Gard'Daemon\n    99→\n   100→All four Zeniths held simultaneously. [[V-Mardov|V-MORDRED]]'s invasion repelled. The final configuration of Controllers, Champions, and Anchor Wielders activates.\n   101→\n   102→\n   103→## The True History Of Mardov (Extracted from PPTX)\n   104→\n   105→### Slide 1\n   106→\n   107→The True History Of\n   108→\n   109→---\n   110→\n   111→### Slide 2\n   112→\n   113→Year 0 - The Rose of Creation\n   114→\n   115→The Rose of Creation was the solution to the ever present conflict between Gods of Destruction and Gods of Creation. It is the culmination of countless Gods and Goddesses’ life's work, and is thusly an irresistible target for Destruction. One such petal on the Rose belonged to The Raven Queen of Fate who made a Pact with Vecna to twin their worlds to their mutual benefit.\n   116→\n   117→---\n   118→\n   119→### Slide 3\n   120→\n   121→The Death Pact\n   122→\n   123→The shared domain of Death would serve as the cornerstone of their agreement. This Pact is largely a mystery, however their specialties point to some grand revelation that will occur at the Fated time of the Raven Queen’s transition from Life to Death. This harvest of the limitless potential of the regent Goddess would culminate in a shared Secret with Vecna, God of Death and Secrets. The end result is a mutually beneficial understanding of What is Beyond the Death of Gods? Each believing they would best the other at the moment of the revelation as they were better equipped to take the advantage, the Pact was sealed.\n   124→\n   125→Mardov and Vodram, twinned worlds, were formed and with them 4 Zeniths capable of wielding the power of the domain of Death.\n   126→\n   127→---\n   128→\n   129→### Slide 4\n   130→\n   131→Zeniths\n   132→\n   133→From the Mountains to the Forest, in Winter or in Harvest, the Zeniths of power await their Anchor and Heart.\n   134→\n   135→There will be War and Death, Pestilence and Famine, but to Anchor the end of Guard’ Daemon, the battlefield will be tallied, its victor declared.\n   136→\n   137→For they wield a secret from beyond the grave of gods. Even in the fires of the Forge or the shadows of the Wilde, the light of Faith and the hope of Progenitors shall strike their Fated Heart.\n   138→\n   139→---\n   140→\n   141→### Slide 5\n   142→\n   143→Bringer of Oblivion\n   144→\n   145→Asmodeus, Bringer of Oblivion only took 100 years to find Mardov, and 12 months to signal the first Guard’Daemon. However, to truely seal Asmodeus away, Kolyma, the First and Last Curator of Mardov had to sacrifice himself to activate the Zeniths and turn them against Oblivion. Unable to fully destroy the God, Asmodeus was sealed in thirteen amulets, to be scattered to the guardians on the edges of existence.\n   146→\n   147→---\n   148→\n   149→### Slide 6\n   150→\n   151→Inheritors of the Mantle\n   152→\n   153→In the Death of Kolyma, the potential he captured to answer the Curator’s Plea was bestowed in four mantles.\n   154→\n   155→The Alabaster Mantle of Arcane Magic\n   156→\n   157→The Ivy Mantle of Natural Magic\n   158→\n   159→The Palladium Mantle of Divine Magic\n   160→\n   161→The Crystalline Mantle of Mortal Magic\n   162→"}]},"uuid":"472a70db-b7c3-4b9c-8bd8-3533c9efed27","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:58:40.829Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b0c3c2d5-d688-4ebc-ae7a-9a8cd5036505","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"e949c2a6-f1b1-43f8-ba5f-5ce8f24850dc","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a93464a70d5231c6f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_019ziNiVkYRvAsE37bKKDj1g","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore\n     3→title: \"Story Arcs & Narrative Structure\"\n     4→---\n     5→\n     6→# Story Arcs & Narrative Structure\n     7→\n     8→## The Overarching Story\n     9→\n    10→The campaign revolves around [[Mordred|Ramses/Mordred]], whose plan unfolds in three stages:\n    11→\n    12→1. **Gets the Avatar of the [[Raven Queen]] to sacrifice her immortality** -- this creates a rift between [[Mardov]] and [[V-Mardov]] where [[Vecna]]'s Avatar rules\n    13→2. **Collects [[Sabine Ra|Sabine]]'s gifts to control her power** -- originally wanted to resurrect [[Sabine Ra|Sabine]], his lost love, but after her spirit shattered he pivoted to controlling her fragments. Gave up his phylactery to take out the [[Artificer's Guild]], gaining [[Xerxes]]'s body (corrupting the individual beings that make up [[Xerxes]])\n    14→3. **Destroys all the keystones** -- with his newfound body, [[Mordred]] has one goal: bring about the invasion of this realm by V-MORDRED by destroying all the keystones\n    15→\n    16→### The Keystones\n    17→\n    18→| Name | Gemstone | Location | Conflict Type | Horseman/Anchor |\n    19→|------|----------|----------|---------------|-----------------|\n    20→| [[Heart of the Wilde]] | Emerald | [[Forest's Zenith]] | Dungeon | Death |\n    21→| [[Heart of the Forge]] | Garnet | [[Mountain's Zenith]] | Dwarven underdark conflict | War |\n    22→| [[Heart of the Faith]] | Sapphire | [[Winter's Zenith]] | Winter's wasteland | Pestilence |\n    23→| [[Heart of the Progenitors]] | Ruby | [[Harvest's Zenith]] | Cataclysmic, philosopher's stone | Famine |\n    24→\n    25→---\n    26→\n    27→## Act I — The Awakening (Chapters 0-13)\n    28→\n    29→The party is recruited into the [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]]. They encounter [[Mordred]] possessing [[Finkle Hollingsworth]], [[Sabine Ra]] is freed from her phylactery, and crystalline heart fragments are deposited in the party. Three prophecies are received from [[Ruth]], the Hags, and [[Mia]], establishing the four-zenith framework.\n    30→\n    31→**Key beats:**\n    32→- Party death and resurrection by [[Sabine Ra]] (Chapter 2)\n    33→- [[V-Mardov]] rift opens; [[Varis Liadon|Varris]] arrives from the alternate reality (Chapter 6)\n    34→- First, Second, and Third Prophecies received (Chapters 9, 12, 13)\n    35→- [[Mia]] reveals she wiped the party's memories\n    36→\n    37→---\n    38→\n    39→## Act II — The Voyage (Chapters 14-40)\n    40→\n    41→The party sails aboard the [[Thornberry Shade]], encounters the zodiac spirits (who bet their immortality on the champions), explores the [[Tomb of the Last Curator]], and splits into parallel tracks.\n    42→\n    43→**Key beats:**\n    44→- Tiefling prejudice arc; soul glamours for [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] and [[Tesca Duskhallow|Tesca]] (Chapters 17-19)\n    45→- Zodiac spirits revealed; they bet their immortality on the champions (Chapter 23)\n    46→- Tomb of the Last Curator; four zenith hieroglyphics decoded (Chapters 24-25)\n    47→- [[Peraxis Phyrexia]] revealed; [[Reynaldine]]'s network exposed (Chapters 30-31)\n    48→- [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]]'s letter found inside [[Daina Fireforge|Daina]]'s axe; [[forgesworn]] lore revealed (Chapter 34)\n    49→\n    50→---\n    51→\n    52→## Act III — The Escalation (Chapters 41-86)\n    53→\n    54→Campaign splits into parallel tracks. Individual character arcs deepen.\n    55→\n    56→### The Cloud City Arc ([[windhaven]])\n    57→\n    58→The party arrives in [[windhaven]] via a portal from the [[Kraken of the Sand Seas]]. Key events:\n    59→\n    60→1. **[[Tiberius D. Rex]]** receives a premonition of the party's arrival\n    61→2. **Research at the Windmill Willow**: [[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]] researches the zodiacs (astral chart showing the 12 animals, one per month); [[Tesca Duskhallow|Tesca]] researches the [[Diadem of Dawn]]; [[Randal]] researches the gods\n    62→3. **[[Leonidas of Summer's Last Light]]** puts the party on trial in his sphinx demi-plane:\n    63→   - [[Varis Liadon|Varys]] requests help against [[Vecna]], is granted a vision of [[Daina Fireforge|Daina]] grabbing the [[Heart of the Forge]] and jumping into lava\n    64→   - [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] requests help to save [[Xyn]], meets [[Juliannia of the Summer Court]] -- her real patron\n    65→   - [[Fen Darkeyes|Fen]] wishes he and his brother never fell under the Aboleth's influence; wish granted\n    66→   - [[Sprocket Overspark|Sprocket]] alone is deemed unworthy (meaning unknown)\n    67→   - [[Tiberius D. Rex|Tiberious]] has undergone this process 75 times, making him far older than the 76 he appears\n    68→4. **The Cabal of Horns**: [[Randal]] and [[Tesca Duskhallow|Tesca]] discover an underground tiefling society led by [[Winnipeg Redvine]], uncovering a child-kidnapping plot by Lady [[Mathia Nuum]]\n    69→5. **Reynaldine's Demand**: The party encounters [[Reynaldine|Reynaldine of the Autumn Court]] collecting Elemental Seals. [[Kelik Temptus]] dies when absorbed into his amulet. [[Reynaldine]] already has Fire and Earth seals; tasks the party with retrieving the Water amulet from [[Lapis Poseidon]]\n    70→6. **[[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]]'s Stag Form**: Discovers her stag form resembles [[Shi-Shi-Gami|Shi Shi Gami]], with antlers forming the [[Zodiac Locket]]\n    71→7. **Cloud City Falling**: The party fights to save Cloud City, encounters [[Zepheeron the Bronze]], battles at Jackfall Landing, and is rescued by [[Captain Liza]] on the [[Thornberry Wilde]]\n    72→8. **[[Reynaldine]]'s True Nature Revealed**: [[Randal]] uses Legend Lore and discovers [[Reynaldine]] is actually 4 beings: [[Juliannia of the Summer Court]], [[Graz'zt|Grazz't the Demon Prince]], [[Gabriel the Kind]] (Archangel), and [[Reynaldine|Reynardine, Prince of Harvest]]\n    73→9. **[[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] meets [[Xyn]]**: Via Lorelai's magical broach, the twin sisters finally communicate. [[Xyn]] reveals [[Gabriel the Kind]] is her patron (lives in teddy bear \"Mr. Happy\"), that the [[Mountain's Zenith]] opening released [[Barachiel the Righteous]], and that [[Euclid]]'s book *The Origin of Genasi and the Rebirth of the Progenitors* is the key to the [[Harvest's Zenith]]\n    74→10. **The Harvest's Zenith spotted**: [[Sprocket Overspark|Sprocket]] touches the satellite and sees a hanging upside-down city with the [[Harvest's Zenith]] -- a dual pyramid with 7 floors all rotating out of phase\n    75→\n    76→---\n    77→\n    78→## Act IV — The Endgame (Chapters 87-136)\n    79→\n    80→The threads converge. Character arcs resolve. Gods die. The world changes.\n    81→\n    82→**Key beats:**\n    83→- Second Reunion; TPK and revival via myconid network (Chapter 103)\n    84→- [[Reginald Providence]] killed; [[Anchor of War]] silenced; Therzadun thwarted (Chapters 104-105)\n    85→- Declaration of War on gods; [[Peraxis Phyrexia|Peraxis]] reveals complex motivations (Chapter 106)\n    86→- Ragnarok; Warforged awakening; [[Irethia]] revealed as aasimar (Chapter 107)\n    87→- [[lolth]]'s \"Two Truths and a Lie\" (Chapter 108)\n    88→- [[Asmodeus]] killed by [[Josh Coolbreeze]]; three specters escape, one enters [[Nocturnus Wayne]] (Chapter 136)\n    89→- [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] becomes deity of [[Mardov]]'s [[Feywild]] via Stone Table sacrifice (Chapter 136)\n    90→\n    91→---\n    92→\n    93→## Global Conflicts\n    94→\n    95→### The Orc-Human War ([[DawnHaven]])\n    96→\n    97→**Location:** [[dawnpass]]/[[windhaven]]/[[Eavallan]] vs [[Stra'Franar]]/[[Fae'ish]]/[[Tygras Logath]]\n    98→\n    99→**Reveal:** At the [[Magister's Circle]], an envoy from [[dawnpass]] (notably containing zero Half-Orcs -- there have been none in the entire game) requests aid against an army of sorcerers wielding draconic-blood-fueled destruction similar to the Tiefling army that destroyed [[uma]].\n   100→\n   101→**Root cause:** The Orc Chieftain and former Gladiator Pit Champion ([[Logath HellEye|Logath]]) made a deal with the same fae demon as [[Tygras]], causing intense bloodlust and madness. This makes him a target for [[Mordred]], who uses Logath's influence to cut off food from the fertile Harvestlands north of [[windhaven]].\n   102→\n   103→**Connection to the Zenith:** This is the location of the [[Anchor of Famine]], tied to the [[Harvest's Zenith]]. The resurgence of Orc sorcerers matches exactly with when it last rained. The [[Matrim Pact]] was between human sailors of old [[uma]], gnomes of [[Fae'ish]], and [[Qual'Xar]] half-elves -- they promised sanctuary atop the [[Maturin Turtle]].\n   104→\n   105→**The Hidden City:** The city is actually a buried pyramid, the great ziggurat housing the [[Harvest's Zenith]]. Inside, [[Tygras]] himself (or the arcane being he became after his failed ascension) has bid his time feeding on internal zenith energies. Destroying his echo rips a hole in the Fae and reveals the fae demon who gave [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] her powers.\n   106→\n   107→**Nyx's Choice:** The demon was the one who made the pact with [[Tygras]], and [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] was his heir. By destroying the last echo of [[Tygras]], future tiefling children can be prevented. [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] inherits the mark of [[Tygras]] (actually Sable's Beacon, an ancient fae marking). She can either rid the world of \"demon children\" or rebuild the lost empire by adding tieflings to the [[Matrim Pact]] and reviving the guardian turtle.\n   108→\n   109→**Reviving the Guardian:** Requires (1) removing the [[Anchor of Famine]] from the top of the zenith (guarded by the Harvest Effigy), (2) returning the [[Heart of the Progenitors]] to the crevasse in the middle of [[Tygras Logath]], (3) speaking the words: *\"On his back all vows are made, a haven for the continent of dawn paid.\"* Upon finishing, the turtle skeleton below the bay is revived -- a massive turtle carrying an Atlantean city, serving as THE landing place between [[Mardov]] and the [[Feywild]].\n   110→\n   111→---\n   112→\n   113→## Character Arc Themes\n   114→\n   115→### [[Sprocket Overspark]] — The Pursuit of Fun\n   116→\n   117→**Question:** Is it possible for responsibility to be fun?\n   118→\n   119→[[Sprocket Overspark|Sprocket]]'s fate as the next Avatar of the [[Raven Queen]] cannot be downplayed. No matter how he tries, this is his overarching conflict: *\"You cannot run from your fate.\"* To learn this is to understand what it means to become the next [[Raven Queen]].\n   120→\n   121→His rise of infamy: Trixie -> [[Thornberry Shade]] -> [[Qual'Xar]] -> [[Magister's Circle]] & [[Conquis Abbey]] -> Entire Material Plane. The deeds he deems \"fun\" provide notoriety, making it harder to be underestimated.\n   122→\n   123→### [[Brigit Wildfire]] — Self-Discovery\n   124→\n   125→**Question:** What makes me special?\n   126→\n   127→[[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]] is a [[Progenitors|Progenitor]] -- one of the original beings created in Eden. This grants her the ability to open the [[Harvest's Zenith]] without destroying the elemental seals. There is no reason for this other than fate -- the Progenitors were fated to return to help stop [[Gard'Daemon]].\n   128→\n   129→### [[Randal]] — Apotheosis and Pacifism\n   130→\n   131→**Question:** What does it take to become a god?\n   132→\n   133→[[Randal]]'s decision to become a pacifist is valiant but ultimately flawed, for he claimed victory while he still has goals outstanding. His ideal: *\"Victory is only satisfying if achieved without violence.\"*\n   134→\n   135→Foils to his pacifism:\n   136→- *\"Violence is Victory\"* -- [[Logath HellEye|Logath]], Orcish Leader\n   137→- *\"Peace is what I will achieve after all my enemies have been defeated by my hand\"* -- [[Evelyandrin of the Spring Court]]\n   138→- *\"Some things are worth fighting for\"* -- [[Reynaldine]]\n   139→- *\"Nothing is worth fighting for, I will endure and adapt\"* -- [[Grumblesin of the Winter Court]]\n   140→\n   141→---\n   142→\n   143→## Player Background Tie-Ins\n   144→\n   145→### [[Sprocket Overspark]]\n   146→- Has a yurt outside [[hinderstap]] village in [[monethern]]\n   147→- Sent by [[lillith]], the Platinum Seat of the Diviners Covenant, to investigate a village where inhabitants are normal by day but ravenous and murderous by night\n   148→- Being killed in the village forces the murdered to stay permanently, reborn each morning (happened to Giddean Clary, Mustard Bard, with his Basset Hound Darling)\n   149→- A portal/wound links [[hinderstap]] to the [[shadowfell]]\n   150→\n   151→### [[Brigit Wildfire]]\n   152→- Criminal contact: [[Firekeeper Morn Loregramar|Firekeeper Lorgrammar]], orc shaman whose people betrayed him and assaulted [[Eavallan]] Forest, [[Stra'Franar]], and Last Tower Bridge\n   153→- She is a [[Progenitors|Progenitor]], one of the original beings created in Eden\n   154→- [[Eavallan]] Forest is of the [[Feywild]], the earliest location where elves first came to [[Mardov]]\n   155→- The original sacking of [[Eavallan]] by the orcs set into motion the steps to open the [[Harvest's Zenith]]\n   156→- Progenitor blood was thought to be in [[uma]], but the bloodline was still strong; [[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]]'s parents were drawn to [[Eavallan]] because it was closest to the Zenith\n   157→\n   158→---\n   159→\n   160→## Story Beats Still To Hit\n   161→\n   162→### [[Randal]] and Apotheosis\n   163→- The gods were the first to uncover universal truths\n   164→- Once a truth is uncovered, Apotheosis becomes possible (see [[lore/cosmology|Cosmology]])\n   165→- [[Reynaldine]]'s plan to collapse all planes is his miracle attempt\n   166→\n   167→### The Raven Queen's Transition\n   168→- The Death Pact between the [[Raven Queen]] and [[Vecna]] hinges on the Raven Queen's transition from Life to Death\n   169→- The harvest of the regent Goddess's limitless potential will culminate in a shared Secret with [[Vecna]]\n   170→- Both believe they will best the other at the moment of revelation\n   171→"}]},"uuid":"57802a72-86e2-4ff2-8562-35e846cf8243","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:58:41.267Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"e949c2a6-f1b1-43f8-ba5f-5ce8f24850dc","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"5b44a58f-ed36-4b27-8f79-f399d687ee3b","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a93464a70d5231c6f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01PZXiu3NxiThxCs1n4dUHSS","type":"tool_result","content":"<persisted-output>\nOutput too large (67.4KB). Full output saved to: /Users/eviemini/.claude/projects/-Users-eviemini-Documents-code-server-manager/e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4/tool-results/toolu_01PZXiu3NxiThxCs1n4dUHSS.txt\n\nPreview (first 2KB):\n     1→---\n     2→type: lore-extraction\n     3→title: \"character-timelines\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/lore-extraction/character-timelines.md\"]\n     5→---\n     6→\n     7→# Mardov Campaign -- Character Timelines\n     8→\n     9→Reconstructed from player notes (Brigit/Liz chapters 49T-136), DM timeline notes (Acts 1-4), and directory/handout evidence. Each character's journey is tracked chronologically through party splits, reunions, and pivotal moments.\n    10→\n    11→> **Confidence Legend**: [HIGH] = directly from player notes or handouts. [MEDIUM] = inferred from structural evidence. [LOW] = reconstructed from context.\n    12→\n    13→---\n    14→\n    15→## BRIGIT (Wild Spirit of Fire)\n    16→\n    17→**Player**: Liz\n    18→**Class**: Druid (Circle of the Moon) / Barbarian multiclass\n    19→**Race**: Changeling/Shapeshifter\n    20→**Key Items**: Anchor of Death (locket), Stag Zodiac\n    21→**Track**: Tieflings -> Distillery -> Good Guys Guild\n    22→\n    23→### Arc Summary\n    24→\n    25→Brigit begins as a chaotic, multi-formed druid struggling with the many voices in her head. She becomes a Progenitor through a ritual in Eden with the Children of the Forest, gains custody of the Anchor of Death in her locket, and carries the Stag Zodiac. Her arc is one of identity -- learning to channel her rage and multiplicity into purpose. She is the Wild Spirit of Fire, and through her shapeshifting, serves as the party's most versatile combatant and emotional anchor. {"parentUuid":"48666b52-d0cf-4132-85c2-67a5c51a36a9","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a93464a70d5231c6f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01FvR1MhGqcfwBWiaxxZSRFr","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: character\n     3→role: npc-major\n     4→name: \"Mordred\"\n     5→aliases: [\"V-Mordred\", \"Finkle (disguise)\"]\n     6→race: \"Unknown (divine agent)\"\n     7→class: \"Necromancer / Vecna's Champion\"\n     8→affiliation: \"[[Vecna Sworn]]\"\n     9→location: \"Various\"\n    10→status: alive\n    11→first_appearance: \"Chapter 0 (disguised as Finkle)\"\n    12→source: [\"NPC Catalog\", \"Player Notes\"]\n    13→art: [\"assets/npc-art-source/Vecna Sworn/Machu/Mordred\"]\n    14→---\n    15→\n    16→## Description\n    17→\n    18→Mordred is Vecna's primary mortal agent and the campaign's first villain. He infiltrated the [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] recruitment disguised as **Finkle Hollingsworth**, a three-foot gnome bureaucrat, orchestrating the pilot's ritual chamber betrayal. His true form is depicted in multiple versions (\"Mordred Completed\", \"New Mordred\"), suggesting ongoing transformation.\n    19→\n    20→> **CRITICAL CORRECTION**: Finkle Hollingsworth is a **Gnome** (NOT a Goblin). He is the disguise Mordred used to infiltrate the Scarlet Tunic Brigade.\n    21→\n    22→## Role in the Campaign\n    23→\n    24→Mordred orchestrated the events of Chapters 0-2: the recruitment, the kobold attacks, the sewer descent, and the ritual chamber. He drained every kobold in the room to create ghouls, revealed three enslaved adventurers, and nearly completed the necromantic ritual before the party shattered the phylactery and freed [[Sabine Ra]].\n    25→\n    26→His plan continues through the [[Vecna Sworn]] organization, with agents at each Zenith working toward Vecna's ascension.\n    27→\n    28→## Faction\n    29→\n    30→[[Vecna Sworn]] -- Machu & Death Zenith division.\n    31→"}]},"uuid":"a8f90eae-4625-4d8f-a3ae-478daf22e676","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:58:48.274Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"48666b52-d0cf-4132-85c2-67a5c51a36a9","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"7baee68b-d244-42e6-8625-db246e7cc2c7","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a93464a70d5231c6f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Eojf3GrhsV9o58HNE2Rzxs","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: faction\n     3→name: \"Scarlet Tunic Brigade\"\n     4→aliases: [\"STB\", \"The Brigade\"]\n     5→category: military\n     6→leader: \"[[Finkle Hollingsworth]] (front) / [[Mordred]] (true)\"\n     7→headquarters: \"Argos\"\n     8→status: active\n     9→source: [\"intrigue-web.md\", \"chapters-act1-2.md\", \"Varys' backstory.txt\"]\n    10→---\n    11→\n    12→# Scarlet Tunic Brigade\n    13→\n    14→## Overview\n    15→\n    16→The Scarlet Tunic Brigade is a private military company that serves as the campaign's opening deception. On the surface, it is a legitimate mercenary organization that recruits adventurers, defends towns, and covers evacuations. In truth, it is a **soul-harvesting operation** run by the lich [[Mordred]] through his vessel [[Finkle Hollingsworth]] (a Gnome, NOT a Goblin).\n    17→\n    18→The Brigade has harvested approximately **2,000 adventurer souls** by luring recruits through [[Jorgen]] and leading them to ritual chambers beneath cities.\n    19→\n    20→## Goals & Motivation\n    21→\n    22→- **Public Face:** Recruit adventurers, defend frontier towns, maintain order\n    23→- **True Purpose:** Harvest souls for [[Mordred]]'s necromantic rituals\n    24→- **End Goal:** Gather enough soul energy to facilitate [[V-Mardov|V-MORDRED]]'s invasion\n    25→\n    26→## Members\n    27→\n    28→### Leadership\n    29→- [[Finkle Hollingsworth]] — COO (Gnome, possessed by [[Mordred]]). Bookish bureaucrat persona. Later freed and becomes a genuine ally.\n    30→- [[Mordred]] / [[Mordred|Ramses]] — The lich controlling the operation. [[Sabine Ra]]'s former teacher.\n    31→\n    32→### Known Agents\n    33→- [[Jorgen]] — Human ranger in a gold tabard. Lure who leads recruits to their deaths. Killed by [[Mordred]], becomes a revenant chanting \"KILL THE GNOME.\"\n    34→- Stromble and Grom — Half-ogre henchmen / bodyguards\n    35→- [[Costa Una]] — Traitor within the Brigade\n    36→- [[dockson]] — An honorable paladin, high-ranking STB general (legitimate side)\n    37→\n    38→### Crime Branch Members\n    39→- Axel Hutch, Hugs Grey, Knifes Henn, Mace Grumm — Muscle\n    40→- Leah Ulstran — Royal Ambassador\n    41→- Rojas Drezel — Mage\n    42→- Slitz Gnard, Lugs — Members\n    43→- The Librarian — Member\n    44→- Drab (5 members) — Cover identities (Entryway, Kitchen, Waiter staff)\n    45→\n    46→## Allies & Enemies\n    47→\n    48→### Allies (True)\n    49→- [[Vecna]] — Ultimate patron\n    50→- [[Mordred]]'s network — [[Golden Reach]] demons, Hag Coven, [[mandu]]\n    51→\n    52→### Allies (Apparent)\n    53→- Frontier towns who rely on STB for defense\n    54→- The party (before the reveal)\n    55→\n    56→### Enemies\n    57→- [[Artificer's Guild]]\n    58→- The party (after Ch. 2)\n    59→\n    60→## History\n    61→\n    62→- Founded by [[Mordred]] as a front for soul harvesting\n    63→- Provided legitimate military service (e.g., covering [[Tower Bridge]] evacuation against [[Ned'Zailok]])\n    64→- [[Varis Liadon|Varis]] enlisted after the Ned'Zailok campaign\n    65→- Chapter 0-2: Party recruited, sewer descent, [[Mordred]] revealed, Brigade exposed\n    66→- Post-reveal: [[Finkle Hollingsworth]] freed from possession, eventually becomes an ally\n    67→\n    68→## Key Events\n    69→\n    70→- **The Sewer Ritual (Ch. 0-2):** Four-colored waterfall chamber beneath Argos. Ritual to harvest souls. [[Sabine Ra]]'s phylactery discovered.\n    71→- **Jorgen's Betrayal (Ch. 0):** Jorgen leads party into the trap, seals them in.\n    72→- **Mordred's Reveal (Ch. 2):** \"2,000 souls, to be exact... They could have flourished if we hadn't betrayed them.\"\n    73→- **The Revenant (Ch. 7-13):** Jorgen's undead form pursues [[Finkle Hollingsworth|Finkle]], chanting \"KILL THE GNOME.\"\n    74→"}]},"uuid":"27e66ce8-419c-4d47-ab65-b2be136c4e6d","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:58:48.737Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"7baee68b-d244-42e6-8625-db246e7cc2c7","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"da8b1065-dc6b-4334-b4e4-c50beeb128ee","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a93464a70d5231c6f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01HgoLQdgM4At9aRJYFHMxeS","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: chapter\n     3→act: 1\n     4→number: 0\n     5→title: \"The Scarlet Tunic Brigade\"\n     6→pcs_present: [\"Stalwart Little\", \"Nocturnus Wayne\", \"Nyx\", \"Daina\", \"Sprocket\", \"Dallor Dallorbill\", \"Bergs\", \"Brigitte\"]\n     7→locations: [\"Argos\", \"Recruit Training Grounds\"]\n     8→npcs_introduced: [\"Finkle Hollingsworth\", \"Stromble\", \"Grom\", \"Jorgen\", \"Slitz Gnard\"]\n     9→items_found: []\n    10→source: \"Act 1&2/Chapter 0/Story.txt\"\n    11→has_recording: false\n    12→has_slideshow: true\n    13→---\n    14→\n    15→# Chapter 0 — The Scarlet Tunic Brigade\n    16→\n    17→## Summary\n    18→\n    19→The campaign opens in the town of [[argos]], newest bastion of the [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]], an adventuring company founded by Chairman [[Slitz Gnard]]. The Brigade was formed after necromancers moved into the Old Forest and traditional tavern-hired adventurers kept disappearing. Over a year, the Brigade fought back the undead hordes until they retreated.\n    20→\n    21→The party arrives for their first assignment as recruits. They are introduced by [[Finkle Hollingsworth]], the Brigade's Chief Operating Officer, a bookish gnome flanked by two half-ogre henchmen, [[stromble]] and [[grom]]. During introductions, Finkle asks mysterious questions about ancient cults in the area, particularly the [[Raven Queen]] cult -- revealing that cult members use necromancy and animancy, view death as a steroid, and mark themselves with symbols under their eyelids.\n    22→\n    23→The introduction is interrupted when [[Jorgen]], a human ranger in a gold tabard, comes sprinting in pursued by flying kobolds. After the party dispatches the kobolds, Finkle assigns them their first mission: descend into the sewers beneath the Middle Ward to reach an ancient ritual site and collect four sources of ancient magic to disperse the kobold infestation.\n    24→\n    25→## Key Events\n    26→\n    27→- Party signs on as [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] recruits\n    28→- [[Finkle Hollingsworth|Finkle]] reveals information about the [[Raven Queen]] cult and excommunicated gods ([[Asmodeus]], [[gruumsh]], [[Raven Queen]], [[Vecna]])\n    29→- Flying kobold attack during orientation\n    30→- [[Jorgen]] arrives having lost his previous party in the sewers\n    31→- First mission assigned: clear the sewer ritual chamber\n    32→\n    33→## Character Moments\n    34→\n    35→- [[Finkle Hollingsworth]] appears bookish and nervous but asks probing, specific questions about cult activity -- foreshadowing his true nature\n    36→- [[Jorgen]] arrives bloodied and terrified, establishing him as the \"expendable guide\" archetype\n    37→\n    38→## Locations Visited\n    39→\n    40→- [[argos]] — town, newest bastion of the Brigade\n    41→- Recruit Training Grounds — [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] orientation site\n    42→\n    43→## NPCs Encountered\n    44→\n    45→- [[Finkle Hollingsworth]] — COO of the [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]]\n    46→- [[stromble]] and [[grom]] — half-ogre henchmen\n    47→- [[Jorgen]] — human ranger in a gold tabard\n    48→- [[Slitz Gnard]] — Chairman of the Brigade (mentioned)\n    49→\n    50→## Items & Loot\n    51→\n    52→- None noted\n    53→\n    54→## DM Notes\n    55→\n    56→- The four excommunicated gods (Asmodeus, Gruumsh, Raven Queen, Vecna) are established early as key cosmic forces\n    57→- The sewer mission is actually a soul-harvesting ritual orchestrated by Finkle/Mordred\n    58→\n    59→## Slideshow\n    60→\n    61→![[assets/chapters-source/Act 1&2/Chapter 0/Slideshow/Argos.jpg]]\n    62→![[assets/chapters-source/Act 1&2/Chapter 0/Slideshow/Gods_unhighlighed.jpg]]\n    63→![[assets/chapters-source/Act 1&2/Chapter 0/Slideshow/The Scarlet Keep.jpg]]\n    64→"}]},"uuid":"07c43f89-2ae6-404d-8c5b-c36236036318","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:58:50.622Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"da8b1065-dc6b-4334-b4e4-c50beeb128ee","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"3317e4e5-a915-4193-977d-b23bcfb9892c","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a93464a70d5231c6f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_016HFDg9YcJqK1rVBgCt4ZGM","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: chapter\n     3→act: 1\n     4→number: 2\n     5→title: \"The Gnome Eff'n Way\"\n     6→pcs_present: [\"Stalwart Little\", \"Nocturnus Wayne\", \"Nyx\", \"Daina\", \"Sprocket\", \"Dallor Dallorbill\", \"Bergs\", \"Brigitte\"]\n     7→locations: [\"Ritual Chamber beneath Argos\"]\n     8→npcs_introduced: [\"Mordred\", \"Sabine Ra\", \"Corramperre\"]\n     9→items_found: [\"Crystalline Hearts\"]\n    10→source: \"Act 1&2/Chapter 2/Story.txt\"\n    11→has_recording: false\n    12→has_slideshow: true\n    13→---\n    14→\n    15→# Chapter 2 — The Gnome Eff'n Way\n    16→\n    17→## Summary\n    18→\n    19→[[Finkle Hollingsworth]] reveals his true nature. His body contorts into a hollow, twisted, gaunt figure -- he is actually [[Mordred]], a lich who has been using the [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] as a soul-harvesting operation. He has collected 2,000 adventurer souls to fuel his power, creating demand for adventure to lure more victims. He commands [[Jorgen]] to be killed for outliving his usefulness.\n    20→\n    21→The party discovers that the large crystalline object in the ritual chamber is a Demi-Lich skull -- [[Sabine Ra]], trapped inside a phylactery. [[Sprocket]] touches it with mage hand and enters a trance. The party manages to shatter the phylactery despite Mordred's fury, freeing Sabine. The freed Demi-Lich turns Mordred's own ghouls against him and forces him to flee.\n    22→\n    23→However, the party falls in combat. In death, they feel an anchor pulling them back. [[Sabine Ra]] sacrifices her newly restored life to revive them, depositing a crystalline heart in each member -- fragments of her own soul. The party awakens in different places and times, reborn at level 2.\n    24→\n    25→## Key Events\n    26→\n    27→- [[Finkle Hollingsworth|Finkle]] revealed as [[Mordred]], an ancient lich\n    28→- [[Mordred]] has harvested 2,000 souls via the [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]]\n    29→- Party manages to shatter [[Sabine Ra]]'s phylactery\n    30→- [[Sabine Ra]] freed, turns on [[Mordred]]\n    31→- Entire party killed by [[Mordred]]\n    32→- [[Sabine Ra|Sabine]] sacrifices herself to resurrect the party with crystalline hearts\n    33→- Party reborn at level 2\n    34→\n    35→## Character Moments\n    36→\n    37→- [[Sprocket]] makes first contact with [[Sabine Ra]] through the phylactery trance\n    38→- The entire party experiences death and rebirth, becoming carriers of Sabine's soul\n    39→\n    40→## Locations Visited\n    41→\n    42→- Ritual Chamber beneath [[argos]]\n    43→\n    44→## NPCs Encountered\n    45→\n    46→- [[Mordred]] (true identity of [[Finkle Hollingsworth]])\n    47→- [[Sabine Ra]] (Demi-Lich, Avatar of the [[Raven Queen]])\n    48→- [[corramperre]]\n    49→- [[Jorgen]] — killed on Mordred's orders\n    50→\n    51→## Items & Loot\n    52→\n    53→- Crystalline Hearts — fragments of [[Sabine Ra]]'s soul, deposited in each party member\n    54→\n    55→## DM Notes\n    56→\n    57→- Mordred is actually Ramses, Sabine's former teacher who forced lichdom upon her\n    58→- The 2,000 harvested souls fuel Mordred's power and his army of ghouls\n    59→- This chapter establishes the campaign's core premise: the party collectively IS Sabine Ra\n    60→\n    61→> [!quote] Mordred\n    62→> \"2000 souls, to be exact... They could have flourished if we hadn't betrayed them. But they will be stronger now that I have risen them in the name of my RAVEN QUEEN.\"\n    63→\n    64→## Slideshow\n    65→\n    66→![[assets/chapters-source/Act 1&2/Chapter 2/Slideshow/the lich.gif]]\n    67→![[assets/chapters-source/Act 1&2/Chapter 2/Slideshow/ritual complete.gif]]\n    68→"}]},"uuid":"b886d5e5-4bd0-4964-b650-cbbcb70090a3","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:58:51.547Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"3317e4e5-a915-4193-977d-b23bcfb9892c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"0d98f150-d2c9-4592-8963-aa4f44af6000","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a93464a70d5231c6f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01M6hwEob82pbwk2kSuDpMWi","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore-extraction\n     3→title: \"Chapter 82T - Brigit s Notes - End Game\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/lore-extraction/Chapter_82T_-_Brigit_s_Notes_-_End_Game.txt\"]\n     5→---\n     6→\n     7→TLDR\n     8→\n     9→We saw the sovereign wood and what the zodiac's add to the story - what Brigit holds in her locket and what it means\n    10→\n    11→We jumped over to the Plane of Water and ran into some shit\n    12→\n    13→We saw The Urchin and a cloaked figure in the distance.\n    14→\n    15→They were attempting to revive Falak the serpent, with Mahen being captured and we got our butts kicked. Brigit and Fen and Varis got downed.\n    16→\n    17→Fen got sucked into the depths with The Sleepless and Sprocket was able to save him from the literal jaws of death.\n    18→\n    19→To save Fen, Mahen gave in and gave her essence within her to the proto-fiendish yanti.\n    20→\n    21→Fen lost his psychic connection to Mahen\n    22→\n    23→Brigit legit died but was revived by a lingering Healing Sprit left by Varis and then was quickly whisked away to Eden\n    24→\n    25→The rest of the party left to go to the next shard of the Elemental Plane of Earth.\n    26→\n    27→Of note - two timers ticking - a pair of fiends making their way across the desert, and the original deal we made with Yuri ~16 hours in the Feywilde.\n    28→\n    29→In the desert - just Varis and Fen\n    30→\n    31→Fiends are coming, and they definitely need some more firepower. What are you going to do?\n    32→\n    33→They definitely mean business.\n    34→\n    35→Varis casts Protection from Evil and Good and comes up with a plan to focus their power on one of them\n    36→\n    37→Maybe they won't follow us through the portal… so maybe they can slip through.\n    38→\n    39→Varis throws up a Wind Wall to try and activate the next portal\n    40→\n    41→What happened to Sprocket and Randall?\n    42→\n    43→They appear in a garden conservatory - a large greenhouse.\n    44→\n    45→In the center is a large glass bell with a singular rose growing out of a petal.\n    46→\n    47→A tree grows around the rose and creates a type of birdcage\n    48→\n    49→Also! All the other contestants of the trials are here, in two circles with the eliminated on the outside.\n    50→\n    51→Reynardine calls out to Randall to say hello - they need something to discuss. And Sprocket can't hear Reynardine, but he can hear everyone else.\n    52→\n    53→They have 2 of the 5 votes. Who are they eliminating? And Sil comes to join them.\n    54→\n    55→Of note: that isn't ashara or hayden, although it does look like them.\n    56→\n    57→Randall is going with whoever Leon doesn't vote for.\n    58→\n    59→Anemone checks in with Sprocket.\n    60→\n    61→They're not really sure where they are and what's going on. But they're close to the ethereal plane\n    62→\n    63→And the wall around them is a force cage\n    64→\n    65→Good news - somewhat protected, bad news - can't hear them and they probably can't hear us.\n    66→\n    67→Randall gestures that he's going to kill Sprocket… maybe.\n    68→\n    69→Lorelei identifies the rose as the Rose of Creation but she's not interested in it and wants to pay attention to Leon. He killed her dad.\n    70→\n    71→Cut to Brigit in a very large garden with Cloyma\n    72→\n    73→And it looks like the place with all the 12 zodiac plants\n    74→\n    75→And other attributations to fire and water, earth and air, all different attributes to gods and goddesses.\n    76→\n    77→In the center - a large pool of water.\n    78→\n    79→Cloyma has an obligation to deal with and has to leave, but before he goes, he warns Brigit not to wander very far.\n    80→\n    81→A large tree appears, a gateway and he steps through it to a very confused circle.\n    82→\n    83→Over the course of the next few hours, Brigit studies the tree and begins to try to understand all of it, also obtaining a small clipping of the tree.\n    84→\n    85→Maybe she can transport to it, but she's uncertain\n    86→\n    87→Back to the desert\n    88→\n    89→A small dust devil is being generated and carving up a small dune.\n    90→\n    91→Also, there's a large shelf of stone - and looks to be hollow\n    92→\n    93→Fen fires an arrow and a hole opens in the stone - revealing a large sandstone tortoise. If the wind was applied, they might be able to drift away more sand and see more.\n    94→\n    95→The portal behind is still pointing to the water place\n    96→\n    97→Large hooves clip across the sandstone above them.\n    98→\n    99→And Varis drops concentration on the Wind Wall to let the sand cover up the hole they entered.\n   100→\n   101→Voices come from above - where did they go, where did this storm come from? But there is something weird here, or there was.\n   102→\n   103→One of the lights above (out of four) lights up.\n   104→\n   105→Varis blows some poison spray at the tortoise statue. And! A portal appears - a swirling bit of cloud\n   106→\n   107→Back to the circle of trial-ese\n   108→\n   109→OMG AN ELEPHANT\n   110→\n   111→The giant elephant in the room, ba dum tskkk\n   112→\n   113→He's on the other side of the barrier, but Sprocket still hears him speak.\n   114→\n   115→Reynardine and Cloyma speak, R attempting to cut the tension.\n   116→\n   117→This is the 6th trial, your guarantee of life is now ended and you can now die.\n   118→\n   119→The eliminated will vote for one of the contestants to be eliminated. Each contestant has five minutes to make their case. You have 10 minutes until the vote begins.\n   120→\n   121→Sprocket asks for volunteers\n   122→\n   123→Anemone doesn't want to die, but she doesn't want to step down.\n   124→\n   125→Lorelei comforts her, and says she'd be willing to step down. There's something important her father wanted and she'll do a good job, but doesn't know what to do.\n   126→\n   127→Ashara and Hayden are silently watching\n   128→\n   129→Hayden comes over, speaks of 7/8/9. and explains that trial 7 is a battle royale, aka thinly veiled threat.\n   130→\n   131→Sprocket looks out to see how the voting might go.\n   132→\n   133→The outer circle\n   134→\n   135→Sil is standing alone, and is unsure of why she's here. She last remembers Ursala and going to visit Leon's study in the manor. She was a part of the tournament, but doesn't remember signing up and says that Ursala would have signed up\n   136→\n   137→(Note - because Regalia took over her, and she only has bits and pieces of what happened)\n   138→\n   139→Randall asks and she tells him immediately to vote out the tiefling, if the tiefling stays, the order will fall into chaos.\n   140→\n   141→Not only is she a tiefling, she seems frail against the others.\n   142→\n   143→She doesn't believe what Reynardine told Randall, that Ashara and Hayden are imposters.\n   144→\n   145→If we vote an imposter out, let's vote out the queen, Ashara. It might be time for new blood.\n   146→\n   147→Leon probably won't vote with them. Maybe Reynardine will. What about Starfire?\n   148→\n   149→Sil and Randall go to talk to Starfire, the mute, elven woman. She steps away from them and begins signing.\n   150→\n   151→Randall asks and she points towards Lorelei.\n   152→\n   153→Randall gathers from context that because she was eliminated first, she wants to vote out the person that got farther than her and didn't have to deal with the trials she did.\n   154→\n   155→Leon's voting for Lorelei\n   156→\n   157→And Reynardine promises to vote with Randall.\n   158→\n   159→The fall/autumn - Ashara.\n   160→\n   161→But with that, Starfire puts a hand up and says not to vote for Ashara, strikes a line through her neck.\n   162→\n   163→And from Reynardine, Hayden is going to be a tough combatant, so maybe we help out our friends.\n   164→\n   165→And Sil agrees. Dope.\n   166→\n   167→To the Garden of Eden\n   168→\n   169→4 different magics to check out.\n   170→\n   171→Natural - zodiacs, elemental and a redwood forest\n   172→\n   173→Through the redwoods, heading on the path of least resistance towards where the elements are pulling\n   174→\n   175→There's markings on the trees, not made with a tool, but made on purpose and it's leading it outwards.\n   176→\n   177→On the edge of the forest, is a biodome - a triangular glass structure marking the garden\n   178→\n   179→Looking back, Brigit can see all the markings lining up on the edges of the trees and the markings look like cave paintings done by children.\n   180→\n   181→NAT 20\n   182→\n   183→There's a depression that opens up in the floor of the forest that leads downward - a circle of stones.\n   184→\n   185→Brigit heads downward into the circle of stones, and sees iconography all along the cave walls\n   186→\n   187→At the end, there's a familiar face - Blossom, one of the children of the forest.\n   188→\n   189→She doesn't look childlike, just carries herself in a much wiser fashion.\n   190→\n   191→She calls out to Brigit, says hello. This is the place where the children of the forest come where they die. She is getting gaunt so she should be reborn soon.\n   192→\n   193→Has she come here to become a progenitor.\n   194→\n   195→YES.\n   196→\n   197→She recognizes Brigit's last name kind of - wild spirit of fire…. And knew her name despite not meeting her before.\n   198→\n   199→To the air shard\n   200→\n   201→Yet again there's a dais surrounded by elements - in this time air.\n   202→\n   203→The wild spirit of Mahen flies all around the shard even though Fen can't connect to her anymore\n   204→\n   205→There's a body on the floor of the dais.\n   206→\n   207→Varis flies around and watches Fen jump from platform to platform. Ready to give a hand if needed.\n   208→\n   209→They both successfully hopscotch to the dais\n   210→\n   211→And in the middle, there's a wound in the clouds almost that hasn't healed yet.\n   212→\n   213→And there's a city hanging downward with a soft amber light at the tallest tower. There are no other people around them yet.\n   214→\n   215→And there's one figure standing out with those fighting above them - maybe Gabriel\n   216→\n   217→The body on the dais is a bat-winged demon devil.\n   218→\n   219→Seems like his necromantic tribal green tattoo is keeping him breathing for now.\n   220→\n   221→And there's a small note - made out to Varis and associates, from Reynardine. He's tried to clear the way here but wishes them luck dealing with Gabriel and the swarm of demons and devils.\n   222→\n   223→Varis blows air at the dais, the surface bends and absorbs the poison, and then a big face appears within the dais. GARTH AND YURI APPEAR\n   224→\n   225→Time for the deal to end. The trial is beginning and it's been 16 hours.\n   226→\n   227→That does not look good over there, good thing you're headed elsewhere…\n   228→\n   229→Fen points out that since Brigit modified his deal, he can choose to leave or to stay.\n   230→\n   231→And Varis was part of the modified deal?\n   232→\n   233→Fen leaves it up to Varis. He's the one being compelled by his patron\n   234→\n   235→Impatiently, Yuri disappears for now while they make their decision.\n   236→\n   237→Varis makes the decision to leave and not follow through with the suicide mission right now.\n   238→\n   239→Fen calls out to Yuri and Garth to leave, expecting some grief, but Garth's hands grab the two of them through the basin.\n   240→\n   241→The 10 minutes are up\n   242→\n   243→The barrier between the two circles dissipates\n   244→\n   245→Sprocket speaks with Randall…\n   246→\n   247→Sprocket would choose Ashara or Hayden, but Randall and co are going with Hayden because he's a buff boi.\n   248→\n   249→Cloyma points out Sprocket has 2 minutes remaining\n   250→\n   251→So he quickly pleads his case. He's not sure if he's the right guy, but he made a promise to be here and try his best.\n   252→\n   253→Lorelei\n   254→\n   255→Lorelei's father was Umbara Populai, a spirit called back to this plane to influence this race, and is the reason she's here.\n   256→\n   257→She would take this responsibility seriously an enact change. It's about time that someone other than a wizard took control. And it's time to stop ignoring wild magic, sorcerer's who's magic traces through their bloodline.\n   258→\n   259→Things will change and the city will open up to the world\n   260→\n   261→Anemone\n   262→\n   263→She points out how weird it is that we're the only ones here, that it's so decoupled from the city itself.\n   264→\n   265→Anyone should be able to enter into this tournament. But what happens when someone who doesn't have magic gets this far and has to bear this burden.\n   266→\n   267→I don't care about your opinion, (to the masses). You don't get a vote and whether you like it or not, I'm going to get to the end and fix the magister's circle\n   268→\n   269→Hayden\n   270→\n   271→<spits at reynardine>\n   272→\n   273→It doesn't matter. I'm not Hayden Dar. I am a construct from the Feywilde representing Summer. Two of us have made it this far and once we win, we'll have the key their require.\n   274→\n   275→Vote as you wish.\n   276→\n   277→Hayden's last act was valiant, despite being misguided. You won't see him anytime soon.\n   278→\n   279→Ashara\n   280→\n   281→Is also not who she looks like.\n   282→\n   283→She thanks Sprocket and Randall in their contribution for her search of Irethia. They've tracked her down.\n   284→\n   285→War. Famine. Pestilence and most importantly, Death will find you all.\n   286→\n   287→Time to vote\n   288→\n   289→Leon - Lorelei\n   290→\n   291→Randall - Hayden\n   292→\n   293→Reynardine - Hayden\n   294→\n   295→Starbound - Ashara\n   296→\n   297→Sil - Hayden\n   298→\n   299→The lights flicker and only the people left in the trial is left standing\n   300→\n   301→Randall\n   302→\n   303→Appears at the Tomb of the Last curator. Manitou is here and hands back the rod of paradise to Randall, finishing their deal\n   304→\n   305→Varis and Fen appear through a blue barrier\n   306→\n   307→They're probably gonna need backup next time\n   308→\n   309→Reynardine leaves and then Varis feels a pull on his bow that he's heading to the direction of Dawnpass\n   310→\n   311→Back in Eden\n   312→\n   313→There's a shamanistic ritual laid out. All the spirits are jamming it out in a drum circle\n   314→\n   315→All around a tree that roots grow upwards to the floor above and downwards below.\n   316→\n   317→Brigit will have to say the oaths, and they've been waiting for her, but they think she'll know the oaths already.\n   318→\n   319→She's been here before.\n   320→\n   321→Down in the leaves, with the tree, she can feel this place in its entirety.\n   322→\n   323→Brigit leaves Eden and finds herself in the Eavallan Forest\n   324→\n   325→A loud cackle rings out - I DID IT. Finkle Hollingsworth.\n   326→\n   327→He looks so emaciated and decrepit. Something is definitely sucking its life force or something.\n   328→\n   329→He's been looking for me, she has the locket.\n   330→\n   331→The Anchor of Death.\n   332→\n   333→He's got the Heart of the Wild, that bastard stole it.\n   334→\n   335→She appeared in her circle. The place from way back, and it's why she came here.\n   336→\n   337→It also appears that it's been messed with. She's in a dreamcatcher of sorts.\n   338→\n   339→Tries to deceive him and get info\n   340→\n   341→He's working with Leon\n   342→\n   343→He seems to be talking to someone that isn't there\n   344→\n   345→She breaks the dreamcatcher below her with Dispel Magic and turns into a Fire Elemental\n   346→\n   347→He tries to hold her but fails\n   348→\n   349→She runs over to him and taps him twice\n   350→\n   351→Misses once, but hits the second.\n   352→\n   353→And it almost seems like something else is controlling him.\n   354→\n   355→The sprit said he wouldn't be hurt\n   356→\n   357→Back with the trial-ers\n   358→\n   359→It's 2v2 battle.\n   360→\n   361→If you win, you'll select one of the losing team to leave\n   362→\n   363→Sprocket partners with Ashara and winks at the other two. And Lorelei and Anemone partner together.\n   364→\n   365→To another tournament battleground\n   366→\n   367→BATTLE MUSIC\n   368→\n   369→Lorelei holds for now\n   370→\n   371→Anemone uses her Mantle and casts command on \"Ashara\" - SURRENDER\n   372→\n   373→But she saves, and a voice not her own says 'NEVER'.\n   374→\n   375→Anemone tries to Firestorm\n   376→\n   377→Ashara counterspells, but it fails!\n   378→\n   379→One cube directly on her\n   380→\n   381→She seems happy and enjoying the firey bath sent her way\n   382→\n   383→Ashara casts incendiary cloud at the other team and spend Sorcerer points to heighten in, giving Anemone disadvantage\n   384→\n   385→Lorelei animates the statue aligning the bridge to reach out its hand and block the cloud\n   386→\n   387→It still happens, but doesn't hit directly.\n   388→\n   389→And Ashara flies a bit out of the way\n   390→\n   391→Sprocket makes somatic gestures in the way of the other team but at the last second, Dominate Person's Ashara.\n   392→\n   393→AND A PORTENT.\n   394→\n   395→FAILS.\n   396→\n   397→She's caught by surprise and completely dominated. NOICE\n   398→\n   399→\"Surrender this fight\"\n   400→\n   401→And then moves away. Surrenders himself and sits on the ground.\n   402→\n   403→Lorelei waits, accepts the surrender graciously\n   404→\n   405→Anemone scuttles away from the fire and then waits\n   406→\n   407→Ashara's fire begins spreading but she sits. And in the flattest, most dead-pan voice: 'I yield'\n   408→\n   409→The bridge collapses, the vines snap away, Cloyma arises from the cavern below in his true size, taller than the two statues and congratulates the two victors.\n   410→\n   411→And Ashara is eliminated!\n   412→\n   413→And everyone leaves to go to the next trial\n   414→\n   415→Back to where Varis and Fen and Randall are\n   416→\n   417→Lots of the other people from before the fifth trial are here.\n   418→\n   419→Goodbean talks to Randall and asks for passage across the sea.\n   420→\n   421→He wants to go back to an isle and reclaim something of his he left before it sinks away.\n   422→\n   423→In the sands seas, Randall saw a space ship in Oswald's study\n   424→\n   425→There is another problem, he's tried this before and the serpent in the serpent sea has been causing difficulties.\n   426→\n   427→And it's been more treacherous since our activities in WindHaven.\n   428→\n   429→Fen sees a newcomer, a well-dressed man, that walks past him and feels a burning from serpent's bane.\n   430→\n   431→He's been sent here as a messenger and there's a siege at our meeting place. He's wondering if we can move our meeting place.\n   432→\n   433→And he notices that Fen's eyes and his bond changed.\n   434→\n   435→Name the meeting spot, they grow tired of waiting. It might not be them sieging us. Golden Tooth village in 24 hours, bring the girl.\n   436→\n   437→Reynardine and Varis. He figured out the Eladrin thing. A key moment - none of the Eladrin were willing to keep up the charade once they were ousted. 3/4 (zeniths, eladrin, what?)\n   438→\n   439→3 zeniths are held by our enemy. The fourth is headed to the winter zenith. Irethia has been taken by the enemy - it's part of the 12th step process.\n   440→\n   441→The winter zenith requires the heart of the faith, so we need the heart of the faith.\n   442→\n   443→Our group has keys - 2/3 and we're doing a pretty good job keeping them safe, but we're on the same team now. We need to get the fourth.\n   444→\n   445→Or the anchor of pestilence. Either will keep the zenith safe and in our control.\n   446→\n   447→But there's this pesky kid, Evan, a white dragon, it's been very difficult to track him down, but Stalwart and his brother Boris have been looking for him and have come the closest.\n   448→\n   449→Abashai, the fiends, the 13 damned. 3 of them are Abashai on loan from Tiamat and Asmodeus. If they get Evan or the rest of the draconic bloodline, they'll bring Tiamat here.\n   450→\n   451→Trust no one but the metallics. Bahamut is good for us.\n   452→\n   453→He's going to go make sure the seals stay hidden and stay difficult for fiends to understand.\n   454→\n   455→About Varis' heart - his crystalline one.\n   456→\n   457→He can portal, due to his heart apparently.\n   458→\n   459→Whatever Mordred's plan is for the crystalline heart, we'll thwart him.\n   460→\n   461→And R reveals that a god brought him back from death and saved him and set him on his path.\n   462→\n   463→And Varis storms off.\n   464→\n   465→MORE BATTLE MUSIC\n   466→\n   467→After being set on fire, Finkle casts Energy drain and barely touches the fire.\n   468→\n   469→He moves away, gets hit by an attack of opportunity\n   470→\n   471→Cackles again, but doesn't hit Brigit\n   472→\n   473→Brigit flies up to Finkle, but misses. AND GOES INTO A RAGE\n   474→\n   475→Brigit hits out, misses\n   476→\n   477→Finkle tries to curse, but misses.\n   478→\n   479→He says that she's not like the other children they've summoned here. It's not working.\n   480→\n   481→He pulls back his left eye, a giant green gem. Tries to hit Brigit, but it fails\n   482→\n   483→He's back against the tree, bites his own tongue and says something\n   484→\n   485→Brigit hits him and it seems like he's waiting for something\n   486→\n   487→He tree strides through the tree\n   488→\n   489→Still alive.\n   490→\n   491→Brigit jumps in through him ~40 secs of rage left. Lands in the temple of life and death in Argos\n   492→\n   493→Back in Eden\n   494→\n   495→For the last trials, we focus on what you have mastered.\n   496→\n   497→Trial 8 - reveal one discovery that has not been known before. A spell, or something to reveals the hidden or lost.\n   498→\n   499→Cloyma chooses the worst to be eliminated.\n   500→\n   501→Lorelei calls a recess - asks if Sprocket actually wants this, but he's celebrating because no matter who wins, he's happy.\n   502→\n   503→Sprocket's gonna throw in the towel, saying that Anemone and Lorelei would be better than him.\n   504→\n   505→Sprocket - pulls out a bonsai tree, tree. A pacifist Awakened Shrub. That becomes an awakened, sentient shrub.\n   506→\n   507→Cloyma is actually pretty stoked about it. It can dance!\n   508→\n   509→Lorelei has a secret - long ago her family cheated in a competition with a feylord. The Westrun's were cursed with undeath and inadvertently set them on their path to destruction at the hand of necromancers (the corpseflowers). Lorelei gave everything up and now her childhood home is an orphanage.\n   510→\n   511→Anemone initially gives up, but Sprocket pushes her to try more. Why did her sister become a necromancer? It's her fault. She failed her. She modified her own memory to forget the necromancy spells.\n   512→\n   513→She raised her mount from the dead, her peryton, spectre.\n   514→\n   515→Sprocket helps her not be so ashamed. But she's sorry to not help with the plan, she's happy with either of them becoming the Alabaster's seat.\n   516→\n   517→Lorelei is eliminated.\n   518→\n   519→He raises up the tree from the center of the fountain, with the rose, that has completely bloomed, much much bigger than the fountain.\n   520→\n   521→This is the magnitude of the Alabaster Mantle.\n   522→\n   523→The final trial\n   524→\n   525→Who can bear its burden longer. Because when it is done, you will perish and become part of it.\n   526→\n   527→Oh no. Anemone doesn't know that the last seat was gonna die. That he wasn't murdered.\n   528→\n   529→<insert freak out>\n   530→\n   531→Sprocket tells her about all of the good things she can accomplish\n   532→\n   533→When you give up and can't bear the mantle, that's when it's over.\n   534→\n   535→Anemone modifies Sprocket's memory.\n   536→\n   537→Anemone is the Alabaster Seat.\n   538→\n   539→Post-trials\n   540→\n   541→Sprocket's the Platinum seat of Divination.\n   542→\n   543→Randall's the Head Musician\n   544→\n   545→Bardic college is joining the magister's circle\n   546→"}]},"uuid":"6384fc39-6660-4aad-b680-57ed63b12695","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:58:56.474Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"0d98f150-d2c9-4592-8963-aa4f44af6000","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"c28f7d97-8291-4fee-bf2c-229d740e5139","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a93464a70d5231c6f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01WMDUcQTctqL7NengcPaWMd","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: player-note\n     3→chapter: 82\n     4→author: \"Brigit\"\n     5→title: \"End Game\"\n     6→group: \"T\"\n     7→source: [\"NFS/Mardov Dnd Reference Guide/Chapter 82T - Brigit_s Notes - End Game.docx\"]\n     8→---\n     9→\n    10→# Chapter 82T: End Game\n    11→\n    12→*Notes by Brigit*\n    13→\n    14→- TLDR\n    15→\n    16→  - We saw the sovereign wood and what the zodiac\\'s add to the story - what Brigit holds in her locket and what it means\n    17→\n    18→  - We jumped over to the Plane of Water and ran into some shit\n    19→\n    20→  - We saw The Urchin and a cloaked figure in the distance.\n    21→\n    22→  - They were attempting to revive Falak the serpent, with Mahen being captured and we got our butts kicked. Brigit and Fen and Varis got downed.\n    23→\n    24→  - Fen got sucked into the depths with The Sleepless and Sprocket was able to save him from the literal jaws of death.\n    25→\n    26→  - To save Fen, Mahen gave in and gave her essence within her to the proto-fiendish yanti.\n    27→\n    28→  - Fen lost his psychic connection to Mahen\n    29→\n    30→  - Brigit legit died but was revived by a lingering Healing Sprit left by Varis and then was quickly whisked away to Eden\n    31→\n    32→  - The rest of the party left to go to the next shard of the Elemental Plane of Earth.\n    33→\n    34→  - Of note - two timers ticking - a pair of fiends making their way across the desert, and the original deal we made with Yuri \\~16 hours in the Feywilde.\n    35→\n    36→- In the desert - just Varis and Fen\n    37→\n    38→  - Fiends are coming, and they definitely need some more firepower. What are you going to do?\n    39→\n    40→  - They definitely mean business.\n    41→\n    42→  - Varis casts Protection from Evil and Good and comes up with a plan to focus their power on one of them\n    43→\n    44→  - Maybe they won\\'t follow us through the portal... so maybe they can slip through.\n    45→\n    46→  - Varis throws up a Wind Wall to try and activate the next portal\n    47→\n    48→- What happened to Sprocket and Randall?\n    49→\n    50→  - They appear in a garden conservatory - a large greenhouse.\n    51→\n    52→  - In the center is a large glass bell with a singular rose growing out of a petal.\n    53→\n    54→  - A tree grows around the rose and creates a type of birdcage\n    55→\n    56→  - Also! {"parentUuid":"86483865-6866-439f-a2af-33983955ed5c","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a93464a70d5231c6f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01CUgxuZn41dynFQUPAybX3b","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore-extraction\n     3→title: \"character-timelines\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/lore-extraction/character-timelines.md\"]\n     5→---\n     6→\n     7→# Mardov Campaign -- Character Timelines\n     8→\n     9→Reconstructed from player notes (Brigit/Liz chapters 49T-136), DM timeline notes (Acts 1-4), and directory/handout evidence. Each character's journey is tracked chronologically through party splits, reunions, and pivotal moments.\n    10→\n    11→> **Confidence Legend**: [HIGH] = directly from player notes or handouts. [MEDIUM] = inferred from structural evidence. [LOW] = reconstructed from context.\n    12→\n    13→---\n    14→\n    15→## BRIGIT (Wild Spirit of Fire)\n    16→\n    17→**Player**: Liz\n    18→**Class**: Druid (Circle of the Moon) / Barbarian multiclass\n    19→**Race**: Changeling/Shapeshifter\n    20→**Key Items**: Anchor of Death (locket), Stag Zodiac\n    21→**Track**: Tieflings -> Distillery -> Good Guys Guild\n    22→\n    23→### Arc Summary\n    24→\n    25→Brigit begins as a chaotic, multi-formed druid struggling with the many voices in her head. She becomes a Progenitor through a ritual in Eden with the Children of the Forest, gains custody of the Anchor of Death in her locket, and carries the Stag Zodiac. Her arc is one of identity -- learning to channel her rage and multiplicity into purpose. She is the Wild Spirit of Fire, and through her shapeshifting, serves as the party's most versatile combatant and emotional anchor. By Act 4 she has ascended the locket, lost her elemental powers during the Harvest Zenith battle, and must find her way forward without her primary coping mechanism.\n    26→\n    27→### Timeline\n    28→\n    29→| Chapter | Event | Significance |\n    30→|---------|-------|-------------|\n    31→| 0-2 | Joins the party; party killed and resurrected by Sabine Ra | [HIGH] Establishes the campaign's death/rebirth theme |\n    32→| 5-7 | Hall of the Raven; early adventures | [MEDIUM] Party cohesion building |\n    33→| 13 | Liberation of Argos | [MEDIUM] First major quest completion |\n    34→| 30-37 | Underdark descent | [MEDIUM] Party faces darkness together |\n    35→| 42 | Campaign splits -- Brigit goes with Tieflings track | [MEDIUM] Party division begins |\n    36→| 49T | Cloud City heist: summons 8 boars to rescue Randall from casino, discovers fiends beneath The Roost, Fen is charmed by Minnie the succubus | [HIGH] First Cloud City arc; Brigit shows tactical creativity |\n    37→| 64T | Harvest Festival preparations: brews beer and wine with the party (Harvest Day IPA, Return of Summer Marigold Wine), creates bottle labels | [HIGH] Distillery business origins |\n    38→| 64T | Discovers drab with sun tattoo, takes pendant | [HIGH] Investigating the drab plague |\n    39→| 69T | Battles the 6-fingered Necromancer (Ned'Zailok) at the Westrun scarecrow farm, fights to burn effigies and save zodiac spirits | [HIGH] First direct confrontation with major villain |\n    40→| 69T | Dies in battle, revived by Varis' lingering Healing Spirit | [HIGH] First death (not counting Ch 2) |\n    41→| 69T | Whisked to Eden after revival | [HIGH] First contact with progenitor realm |\n    42→| 72T-76T | Alabaster Seat tournament: teams with Anemone, fights beholders, navigates political intrigue, rescues Ruth and Sheila from Mage's Prison | [HIGH] Distillery political arc |\n    43→| 77T | Trial of Sprocket: testifies in court, casts Greater Restoration on Lilith to break spellbook curse | [HIGH] Proves loyalty to party |\n    44→| 78T | Beholder trial: teams with Anemone and Olivia, Anemone destroys the beholder in 2 rounds | [HIGH] Combat prowess of allies |\n    45→| 79T | Feywilde journey: attends Summer Court tea party, votes in the House of Blood | [HIGH] Feywilde politics |\n    46→| 82T | Becomes a Progenitor through ritual in Eden with Children of the Forest | [HIGH] Major transformation -- one of the campaign's pivotal character moments |\n    47→| 82T | Finds Finkle Hollingsworth in the Eavallan Forest, fights him, chases through tree stride to Temple of Life and Death in Argos | [HIGH] Finkle has Heart of the Wilde in his eye |\n    48→| 83T | Flies 200 miles to Distillery as Quetzalcoatl to retrieve bag of holding, flies back | [HIGH] Shows dedication and power |\n    49→| 84T | Discovers underground city beneath Argos, rescues 4 Children of the Forest (total 8/12 saved) | [HIGH] Children of the Forest quest progress |\n    50→| 85T | Battle of the Distillery: turns into dragon, uses Call Lightning against Dallor | [HIGH] Major battle |\n    51→| 86 | REUNION: full party together, fights Dallor, Sprig shatters the crystalline heart | [HIGH] End of Act 3 split |\n    52→| 87T | Performs Ritual of Reincarnation for Freld and 6 others; Daina receives Heart of the Mountain | [HIGH] Progenitor powers in action |\n    53→| 88-89 | Mourning period: becomes a wolf, creates barbarian camp, regrows burned forest | [HIGH] Processing grief |\n    54→| 90 | Explores Machu and House of Life and Death; captured briefly, HAL rescues her and Cersei | [HIGH] Temple of Life and Death reconnaissance |\n    55→| 91T-99 | Distillery period: business operations, defense building, STB confrontation | [MEDIUM] Settling and fortifying |\n    56→| 100 | Shadowfell journey -- encounters time dilation | [HIGH] Major planar adventure |\n    57→| 101 | Mountain disappears, summoned to Court of Bones | [HIGH] Escalating cosmic events |\n    58→| 103 | The Reunion (third time) | [HIGH] Party comes together again |\n    59→| 104-105 | Witnesses Anchor of War events, Daina ascends | [HIGH] Supporting role in critical moment 67→| 121 | Stalwart corruption/redemption duels; Brigit ascends the locket (Anchor of Death) | [HIGH] The locket's power is fully realized |\n    68→| 125 | Varis retrieves final Asmodeus seal | [HIGH] Endgame preparation |\n    69→| 127-128 | Conquis Abbey battle: Brigit is cleansed by Narcissa's rapier, goes full rage as Lox, fights temple leaders | [HIGH] \"I live with this shit every goddamn day\" -- iconic rage speech |\n    70→| 128 | Uncleansed after fight; rapier returns to Josh | [HIGH] Cleansing didn't stick; identity remains complex |\n    71→| 130 | Cloud City return: disguises as eagle aaracokra, helps heist Lapis from throne room, frees the 10th Wind | [HIGH] Major heist sequence |\n    72→| 131 | Cloud City continued: creates wall of fire corridors, rescues civilians, turns into giant eagle to carry party | [HIGH] Tactical and heroic |\n    73→| 132 | Stone Table ritual: fights alongside party, casts cone of cold at Gabriel, heals Bergs as zodiac of life | [HIGH] Critical battle |\n    74→| 133 | Harvest: uses scroll of Transport via Plants to reach Conquis Abbey, turns into Quetzalcoatl to reach zenith | [HIGH] Rapid deployment |\n    75→| 135 | Divinity: casts Bestow Curse on Asmodeus, fights in dragon form, casts Haste on self, channels Nivens for stealth | [HIGH] Multi-form combat mastery |\n    76→| 135 | Scries on Forest Zenith anchor Critical intelligence |\n    79→\n    80→### Turning Points\n    81→\n    82→1. **Death and Revival (Ch 69T)**: First real death, revived by party magic. Establishes mortality stakes.\n    83→2. **Becoming a Progenitor (Ch 82T)**: Transforms from adventurer to something more -- connected to the land itself.\n    84→3. **The Rage Speech (Ch 127)**: \"Do you have any idea how many voices exist in my head at any given time?\" -- defines her identity.\n    85→4. **Power Loss (Ch 135-136)**: Elemental magic stripped at Harvest Zenith. The silence in her head is deafening.\n    86→\n    87→### Status (as of Ch 136)\n    88→\n    89→- Alive, powers stripped (no elemental/vegan magic)\n    90→- Anchor of Death ascended but forest zenith situation unclear\n    91→- Locket pulsating abnormally (acrid green vs usual emerald)\n    92→- Must find Forest Zenith and confront zombie Finkle + demi-lich phylactery\n    93→- Needs her powers turned back on\n    94→\n    95→---\n    96→\n    97→## SPROCKET OVERSPARK\n    98→\n    99→**Player**: (unknown)\n   100→**Class**: Wizard (School of Divination)"}]},"uuid":"ece6d067-bdd6-40fd-bbaf-492945874454","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:58:58.809Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"86483865-6866-439f-a2af-33983955ed5c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}
