She had a unique connection with this gem of unimaginable power.\nmardov-vault/sources/dm-notes/dnd overarching story.md:11:MORDRED:\nmardov-vault/sources/dm-notes/dnd overarching story.md:14:\t\t- originally he wanted to resurrect SABINE, his lost love, however after her spirit was shattered he wanted to collect her gifts and control her power (this mirrors V-MORDRED)\nmardov-vault/sources/dm-notes/dnd overarching story.md:16:\t\t- with his newfound body, MORDRED has one goal: bring about the invasion of this realm by V-MORDRED by destroying all the keystones\nmardov-vault/sources/dm-notes/dnd overarching story.md:20:\tHEART OF THE WILDE      <EMERALD>\tFOREST'S ZENITH\t\tdungeon\t\t\t\t\tdeath\nmardov-vault/sources/dm-notes/dnd overarching story.md:35:   GARD'DAEMON IS NEIGH, THE FIRST ZENITH IS RUBBLE,  //first zenith has already been plundered by MORDRED, he already had the heart of the wild and used it, what did he use it for? awakened the forest guardian, this is going to be the big boss in the zenith\nmardov-vault/sources/dm-notes/Prophesies and Zeniths.md:29:- Hearts of Stone: We've seen a heart of stone, the Heart of the Wilde, plus the other slots in the tower of the Red Tunic Brigade. We also know from Tiberious that this stone has been stolen and \"gathered from plunder\"\nmardov-vault/sources/dm-notes/Prophesies and Zeniths.md:51:- The first zenith: Always refers to the Forest Zenith. We're not quite sure how its rubble.\nmardov-vault/sources/dm-notes/Prophesies and Zeniths.md:89:  - Currently we know that Macchu is the \"Forest Zenith\" and Vagna Har is the \"Mountain Zenith\". The other two are still a mystery/we haven't heard about them as much. I would assume that Bynar is the Sea/Ocean Zenith, and then I don't know about Tygras Logar.\nmardov-vault/sources/dm-notes/Prophesies and Zeniths.md:113:- Forest and Sunlight: Finkle will have more information about this.\nmardov-vault/sources/dm-notes/Prophesies and Zeniths.md:115:- Madness Prisoner: Mordred/Ramses and his phylactery.\nmardov-vault/sources/dm-notes/Prophesies and Zeniths.md:117:- Star-crossed three: Sabine, Mordred/Ramses and Xeres. Once we bring their souls/phylacteries to the forest, and complete the trial of the zenith, we will bring peace to them.\nmardov-vault/sources/dm-notes/Villain Ideas.md:33:- Building a tower to the floating Forest Zenith\nmardov-vault/sources/dm-notes/story arch ideas.md:12:sabine's crystaline heart will be removed from the adventurer's and they will no longer be under threat of being spirited away off to V-MORDRED's universe. \nmardov-vault/sources/dm-notes/story arch ideas.md:14:this is the only way to force V-MORDRED back into the realm from which he came. \nmardov-vault/sources/dm-notes/story arch ideas.md:18:\tis a magic sword which has been thrust in the forest's zenith since time memorium. The sword has long since been turned to obsidian through the test of time, \nmardov-vault/sources/dm-notes/Zodiacs and Player Symbolism.md:11:------------------------------------------------- FORREST's ZENITH && ANCHOR OF DEATH && HEART OF THE WILDE -------------------------------------------------\nmardov-vault/sources/dm-notes/Zodiacs and Player Symbolism.md:12:[Omitted long matching line]\nmardov-vault/sources/dm-notes/Zodiacs and Player Symbolism.md:14:[Omitted long matching line]\nmardov-vault/sources/dm-notes/Zodiacs and Player Symbolism.md:51:\t\t- Natural (HEART OF THE WILDE):\nmardov-vault/sources/dm-notes/conflicts.md:14:\t\t    |\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t   | races and ages/sexes however none of them are Orc or Half-Orc, note there have been 0 Half-Orcs in  | This makes him a target for MORDRED and he has used this new influence to create pressure on the peaceful DAWNPASS in an effort to cut off food previously from the fertile Harvestlands north of WINDHAVEN."}]},"uuid":"b0f8ce2d-6cdc-4dee-8478-452d1336b026","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:01.491Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"27d43a1c-a51e-4a1b-bc6e-1e3cb7c28fa3","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"324d5a95-6a6a-4c8d-a97e-5de4d93700b4","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a93464a70d5231c6f","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"\"$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR/.claude/hooks/check-discord-queue.sh\""},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_01XBqZ6xUZouzguTj62VYbje","toolUseID":"toolu_01XBqZ6xUZouzguTj62VYbje","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:01.971Z","uuid":"20af1eaf-cf4c-44ef-a104-55d52f1c06b5","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}
{"parentUuid":"20af1eaf-cf4c-44ef-a104-55d52f1c06b5","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a93464a70d5231c6f","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"callback"},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_01XBqZ6xUZouzguTj62VYbje","toolUseID":"toolu_01XBqZ6xUZouzguTj62VYbje","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:01.971Z","uuid":"b5b9f592-1c3b-4824-bc5e-953337d908e4","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}
{"parentUuid":"324d5a95-6a6a-4c8d-a97e-5de4d93700b4","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a93464a70d5231c6f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01XBqZ6xUZouzguTj62VYbje","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore-extraction\n     3→title: \"story-arc-dm-notes\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/lore-extraction/story-arc-dm-notes.md\"]\n     5→---\n     6→\n     7→# Mardov Story Arc — DM's Meta-Narrative and Plans\n     8→\n     9→*Extracted from DM's personal notes: story arch ideas.txt, dnd overarching story.txt, conflicts.txt, Prophesies and Zeniths.docx, Zodiacs and Player Symbolism.txt, ideas.txt, story idea bites.txt*\n    10→\n    11→---\n    12→\n    13→## The Core Thesis\n    14→\n    15→The entire campaign is built on the concept of **twin souls** — a cosmic mechanic originating from the gods Vecna and Sabine, who were the original twin souls. Vecna's jealousy of Sabine's additional powers (she rules life AND death, making her also the goddess of fate) drives the entire meta-narrative. Every major conflict is a fractal echo of this divine sibling rivalry.\n    16→\n    17→---\n    18→\n    19→## Mordred's Arc (The Primary Antagonist)\n    20→\n    21→### Phase 1: The Lover\n    22→Mordred originally wanted to resurrect Sabine, his lost love. This is the sympathetic core of his villainy.\n    23→\n    24→### Phase 2: The Collector\n    25→After Sabine's spirit was shattered, he pivoted to collecting her gifts and controlling her power. This mirrors what V-MORDRED does in the parallel dimension.\n    26→\n    27→### Phase 3: The Conspirator\n    28→- Convinced the Avatar of the Raven Queen to sacrifice her immortality\n    29→- This sacrifice created a rift between the material world and V-MARDOV (Vecna's domain)\n    30→- Gave up his own phylactery to infiltrate and destroy the Artificer's Guild\n    31→- This granted him Xerxes's body (corrupting the beings that compose Xerxes)\n    32→\n    33→### Phase 4: The Herald\n    34→With his new body, Mordred has a single goal: destroy all keystones to enable V-MORDRED's invasion. He is no longer acting for love but as an agent of the apocalypse.\n    35→\n    36→---\n    37→\n    38→## The Four-Zenith Structure\n    39→\n    40→The DM has designed the campaign as four parallel quest lines, each involving:\n    41→1. A **geographic conflict** (war, famine, pestilence, or systemic corruption)\n    42→2. A **Zenith** to cleanse (ancient nexus of power)\n    43→3. An **Anchor** to recover (weapon tied to a Horseman)\n    44→4. A **Heart** to secure (gemstone keystone)\n    45→5. **Three player characters** whose personal arcs interweave with the conflict\n    46→6. A **final battle station** where one player mans each Zenith\n    47→\n    48→### Arc 1: Harvest's Zenith (Famine) — Tygras Logath / DawnHaven\n    49→\n    50→**The Conflict:** Orc-Human war. Drought has destroyed the Harvestlands. An orc chieftain, corrupted by the same fae demon that empowered Tygras, is besieging DawnPass.\n    51→\n    52→**The Discovery:** The resurgence of orc sorcerers matches to the day with the last time it rained — supernatural famine. The Matrim Pact (humans, gnomes, half-elves united on the Maturin Turtle) holds the key.\n    53→\n    54→**The Dungeon:** The Harvest's Zenith is inside a buried pyramid (the great ziggurat), where Tygras himself still exists as an arcane being, feeding off the Zenith's internal energies.\n    55→\n    56→**The Choice:** After destroying Tygras's echo, Nyx meets the fae demon. She inherits the Mark of Tygras (Sable's Beacon). She can either rid the world of \"demon children\" (end tieflings) or rebuild the lost empire by adding tieflings to the Matrim Pact.\n    57→\n    58→**The Revival:** To revive the Maturin guardian:\n    59→1. Remove the Anchor of Famine from the zenith top (guarded by the Harvest Effigy)\n    60→2. Return the Heart of the Progenitors to the center of Tygras Logath\n    61→3. Speak: \"On his back all vows are made, a haven for the continent of dawn paid.\"\n    62→4. The turtle skeleton beneath the bay revives — a massive turtle carrying an Atlantean city, serving as THE landing point between the material world and the Fae\n    63→\n    64→**Player Arcs:**\n    65→- **Nyx (Owl):** Discovers her patron's last patronee was Tygras. Her sister Xyn's soul was the price. Wields Heart of Progenitors for all warlock rituals.\n    66→- **Irethia (Fox):** Discovers Aasimar vs. Tiefling distinction. Mans the Harvest Zenith in final battle.\n    67→- **Tesca (Mouse):** Discovers the Diadem of Dawn IS the Anchor of Famine — one wish that permanently removes spellcasting. Needed to destroy Tygras's Fae-Machine.\n    68→\n    69→### Arc 2: Winter's Zenith (Pestilence) — Bynar\n    70→\n    71→**The Conflict:** The dragon-kin tribe (Winter's Curators) is ruined. A fae eel serpent has swallowed the Heart of the Faith. Yuan-Ti stole the Anchor of Pestilence.\n    72→\n    73→**The Discovery:** The Sunless Citadel in the Shifting Sands is a Yuan-Ti fortress and shrine to the Serpent's Chosen. There is no Winter's Zenith at the citadel — the real one is within the mountain of Bynar, guarded by the Winter Dragon (White Dragon).\n    74→\n    75→**The Irony:** Stalwart's drive for revenge leads him to the truth. There's no zenith where he expected one, but he finds the Shield of Retribution instead.\n    76→\n    77→**Player Arcs:**\n    78→- **Randal (Turtle):** Must find the pan flute and resurrect the turtle Mia. The pan flute IS the Anchor of Pestilence. Grants anti-inspiration (debuff enemies) and group inspiration.\n    79→- **Stalwart (Mongoose):** Discovers Heart of Faith was stolen by White Dragon. Shield of Retribution + Heart of Faith = channel divine magic without a deity (pure retribution).\n    80→- **Dallor (Serpent):** Determines the Sunless Citadel is a Serpent's Chosen shrine. The ascended will call the serpent to defend her. Mans Winter's Zenith in final battle.\n    81→\n    82→### Arc 3: Mountain's Zenith (War) — Vag Mahar\n    83→\n    84→**The Conflict:** Dwarven civil war. Three Thanes squabble. Three secret police forces (Blackguard, Truth's Light, Silent Flame) are all secretly controlled by a single Mind Flayer.\n    85→\n    86→**The Discovery:** The Mind Flayer wants the Anchor of War (a hammer capable of earthquake-sinking Vag Mahar) to breed better Duergar. The forgotten First Forge must be relit.\n    87→\n    88→**The Resolution:** The bastard Dorrin (connected to Bergs/Daina) is the only one who can build/control Warforged. His golem Roc proves his lineage. He must be crowned Paragon.\n    89→\n    90→**Player Arcs:**\n    91→- **Daina (Wolverine):** Forgelord's daughter, twin soul with Bergs. Wields Anchor of War (half hammer-half axe, half javelin) to harness the First Flame and transform Bergs.\n    92→- **Bergs (Wolf):** Must give up his wild/natural self. Heir to First Forge. Becomes a Warforged powered by Heart of the Forge. When combined with all four Hearts, he's the Voltron power conduit.\n    93→- **Nocturnus (Bat):** Noble nephew to the Underdark vampire queen. Must kill his aunt (who gave her soul to Tiamat via bat-worship ascension). Mans Mountain's Zenith in final war.\n    94→\n    95→### Arc 4: Forest's Zenith (Death) — Machu\n    96→\n    97→**The Conflict:** The king of Machu is trapped in madness. Mordred/Ramses is the \"madness prisoner.\" The Forest's Zenith has already been partially plundered by Mordred (he had the Heart of the Wilde and used it to awaken the forest guardian — the big boss).\n    98→\n    99→**The Discovery:** After defeating the forest guardian, the Heart of the Wilde transports the ENTIRE party to V-MARDOV — inside the Death's Zenith — where they fight Lich Ned'zailok (Keeper of Death's Anchor).\n   100→\n   101→**The Resolution:** Must use a sun-reflecting mirror to access the Zenith. Free the king from madness. Bring peace to the star-crossed three:\n   102→- Xerxes's soul: held prisoner at the bottom of the Zenith\n   103→- Ramses's soul: in Daina's bag, must be returned to the phylactery for cleansing\n   104→- Sabine: appears in the flash as both bodies merge into a demi-lich\n   105→\n   106→**Player Arcs:**\n   107→- **Sprocket (Rabbit):** Vessel for Sabine Ra's return. Gains Sabine's cloak (luck feat: 3 rerolls/long rest), can channel necromancy, wields Anchor of Death in final fight.\n   108→- **Brigitte (Giant Elk):** Collects all 12 zodiac forms. Each grants psychic connection + animal form + ability to locate the zodiac being. Zodiac Locket at bottom of Tomb of the First Curator = unlimited shapeshifts + 2 Wisdom.\n   109→- **Varris (Doe):** Linked to Lich Ned'zailok in V-MARDOV. Sits in Forest's Zenith control room during final fight.\n   110→\n   111→---\n   112→\n   113→## The Prophecy Structure\n   114→\n   115→The DM uses a **layered prophecy system** — three prophecies of increasing specificity, delivered by different sources:\n   116→\n   117→1. **Ruth's Divination** (first, broadest): Establishes the stakes — anchors falling, hearts stolen, Vecna coming, blood star heralding war. Four tarot cards drawn: tiger (wind), forge (creation), holy knight (faith), family (progenitors).\n   118→\n   119→2. **The Hags' Divination** (second, nursery rhyme): \"Double bubble toil and trouble\" — confirms the first zenith (Forest) is already compromised, pestilence is being prepared with children's souls, anchors being lifted and hearts smuggled.\n   120→\n   121→3. **Mia's Divination** (third, specific/actionable): Four verses mapping exactly to four locations with specific conditions for resolution. This is the quest roadmap.\n   122→\n   123→**Design Intent:** Each prophecy is more actionable than the last. Ruth gives the cosmic scale, the Hags give the immediate threat, Mia gives the actual quest structure.\n   124→\n   125→---\n   126→\n   127→## The Final Battle (Guard'Daemon / Armageddon)\n   128→\n   129→**Setup:** V-MORDRED's invasion. The blood star falls. All four Zeniths must be held simultaneously.\n   130→\n   131→**Battle Stations:**\n   132→- Forest's Zenith: **Varris** (control room)\n   133→- Mountain's Zenith: **Nocturnus**\n   134→- Winter's Zenith: **Dallor**\n   135→- Harvest's Zenith: **Irethia**\n   136→\n   137→**Champions (Anchor wielders):**\n   138→- Anchor of Death (sword): **Sprocket**\n   139→- Anchor of War (hammer-axe-javelin): **Daina**\n   140→- Anchor of Pestilence (pan flute): **Randal**\n   141→- Anchor of Famine (Diadem of Dawn / one wish): **Tesca**\n   142→\n   143→**Power Conduit:**\n   144→- **Bergs** as Warforged powered by Heart of the Forge, channeling all four Hearts in a Voltron-style beam convergence\n   145→\n   146→**Heart Champions:**\n   147→- Arcane (Forge): Bergs\n   148→- Radiant (Faith): Stalwart\n   149→- Natural (Wilde): Brigitte\n   150→- Fae (Progenitors): Nyx\n   151→\n   152→---\n   153→\n   154→## Unused Ideas and Story Bites\n   155→\n   156→### The Colosseum City\n   157→A city that forces inhabitants to fight their way to the center circle. Citizens can only be saved by reaching the center and winning single combat. Could be a gladiatorial side-arc or the orc chieftain's domain.\n   158→\n   159→### The Dome\n   160→A giant dome of blackness, open at the top. Party must find a MacGuffin in the dark and make their way out. A dungeon concept — could be deployed anywhere.\n   161→\n   162→### The Underground Crime Network\n   163→- NPC tells party about \"the underworld\" — it's a trap\n   164→- Railcart getaway with bombs in back carts covering tracks\n   165→- The bombs are actually breaches for an invasion\n   166→- **THE PARTY IS FRAMED FOR THE INVASION**\n   167→- This is a fully-formed heist-gone-wrong arc\n   168→\n   169→### Miscellaneous Combat Ideas\n   170→- Misty Step Rogue: Grimnak\n   171→- Badass ally dimension doors in, saves the party, dimension doors out before they come to\n   172→- Battlefield with elemental planes and affinities (environmental combat mechanics)\n   173→\n   174→### The Silver Scales of Bahamut\n   175→Two philosophical factions within Bahamut's priesthood:\n   176→- Those who judge by **Justice** (what is fair by law)\n   177→- Those who judge by **Righteousness** (what is morally correct)\n   178→- The tension: \"All that is Just is not Righteous! All that is Righteous is not Just!\"\n   179→- This could fuel internal conflict within Conquis Abbey\n   180→\n   181→---\n   182→\n   183→## DM Design Patterns\n   184→\n   185→### Thematic Mirroring\n   186→Every level of the narrative mirrors the Vecna/Sabine dynamic:\n   187→- Cosmic: Vecna vs. Sabine (twin souls, jealousy)\n   188→- Mortal: Mordred's love for Sabine → corruption\n   189→- Player: Twin soul mechanics (Daina/Bergs, Nyx/Xyn)\n   190→- Structural: Material plane vs. V-MARDOV\n   191→\n   192→### The Monkey's Paw Principle\n   193→Nothing comes free. Every power has a cost:\n   194→- Magic items have hidden downsides\n   195→- The Diadem of Dawn grants a wish but removes spellcasting forever\n   196→- Nyx gains full warlock power at her sister's cost\n   197→- Bergs must give up his natural self to become Warforged\n   198→\n   199→### Player Agency Through Choice\n   200→The DM consistently designs climactic moments as binary choices:\n   201→- Nyx: End tieflings or rebuild the empire?\n   202→- Tesca: Use the one wish (and lose spellcasting) or find another way?\n   203→- Nocturnus: Kill his aunt or find another path?\n   204→- Each choice reshapes the world\n   205→\n   206→### Four-Part Symmetry\n   207→Everything comes in fours, matching the Horsemen:\n   208→- 4 Zeniths, 4 Hearts, 4 Anchors, 4 Horsemen\n   209→- 4 Eyes of Vecna (Green, Red, Blue, Orange)\n   210→- 4 Sources of Power (Arcane, Radiant, Natural, Fae)\n   211→- 12 players = 3 per Zenith\n   212→- 4 tarot cards in the first divination\n   213→\n   214→---\n   215→\n   216→## Key Lore Details for Adaptation\n   217→\n   218→### The Matrim Pact\n   219→An alliance of three peoples (humans, gnomes, half-elves) who fled the destruction of Uma on the back of the giant turtle Maturin. They built a hidden city — a buried pyramid — that serves as the Harvest's Zenith. The turtle's revival is one of the campaign's most visually spectacular set pieces.\n   220→\n   221→### Tygras and the Tieflings\n   222→Tygras was a tiefling warlord who made a pact with a fae demon. His superweapon destroyed Uma, creating the desert. The Covenants of Magic were created in response. Tygras was sealed in the Harvest's Zenith where he's been feeding off its energy ever since. His legacy: all tieflings are his descendants through fae corruption.\n   223→\n   224→### The Watchful Order\n   225→The magical government/police force. They determine which magic is legal and which creatures live or die. They're a potential ally or obstacle depending on the party's methods.\n   226→\n   227→### Lord Jerefor\n   228→A smuggler in Argos — the suspected smuggler of the Hearts (\"hearts are smuggled\" from the second prophecy).\n   229→\n   230→### The Blood Star\n   231→A recurring astronomical omen. First appeared when Tygras came to power. Will appear again for Guard'Daemon. It physically falls — a meteorite. The last one killed King Thorson Bronzebeard II.\n   232→\n   233→### Sabine Ra\n   234→Will return as a demi-lich through the merging of Ramses and Xerxes in the Forest's Zenith. Sprocket serves as her vessel. She is simultaneously goddess of life, death, and fate — the most powerful entity in the setting, but shattered and scattered.\n   235→\n   236→---\n   237→\n   238→## Map of the Five Key Points\n   239→\n   240→From the Thornberry Shade's map (bubbled up in five places):\n   241→1. **Var'taos Ruins / Valley of Anchors** — Origin of magic\n   242→2. **Vag Mahar** — Dwarven city, Mountain's Zenith\n   243→3. **Machu** — Forest's Zenith\n   244→4. **Bynar** — Winter's Zenith\n   245→5. **Tygras Logath** — Harvest's Zenith (additionally had a burning ember)\n   246→\n   247→These five points form the geographic skeleton of the entire campaign.\n   248→\n   249→"}]},"uuid":"75619322-f274-401f-adf3-bb09cda4c388","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:01.971Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"324d5a95-6a6a-4c8d-a97e-5de4d93700b4","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"c072126f-4956-4929-ae26-e03f6e46868c","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a93464a70d5231c6f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01FET21beYLMHnyyDkonKVXd","type":"tool_result","content":"83-\n84-### Summary Points (from Chapter 1 Summary)\n85-\n86:- DALLOR DALLORBILL swiped a note from Jorgen revealing Finkle had the precise arrival time and names/classes of all recruits\n87-- BRODEN ROOTBEARD received information about the Raven Queen Cult: they used necromancy and animancy as worship, viewed death as a steroid and life as potential energy\n88-- The blood rune puzzle led to a split party -- some ran through the wall, others descended through the floor\n89-- STALWART LITTLE spoke Draconic and kept the kobolds at bay despite their eerie chanting of \"SCARLET, SCARLET, SCARLET\"\n90:- Half the party found Jorgen and Finkle's half-ogre henchmen in a control room pulling levers (priming the ritual chamber), caught off guard\n91-\n92----\n93-\n--\n98-JORGEN struggles while in STALWART's grasp.\n99-JORGEN: \"Let go of me, do you realize what is going on here?\"\n100-\n101:At that moment, from behind the door strolls in FINKLE HOLLINGSWORTH.\n102-\n103:FINKLE: \"It seems this latest batch of fresh souls is proving a bit stronger than the average kobold horde. No matter, I'm confident we can fix them.\"\n104-\n105:[Omitted long matching line]\n106-\n107:FINKLE: \"Kill him: I have all the life energy I need, my undead empire will flourish beneath this city, for the RAVEN QUEEN smiles upon this place.\"\n108-\n109-JORGEN: \"But boss, I've done everything you've asked! I've led countless parties to their deaths down here, why kill me NOW!\"\n110-\n111:FINKLE: \"2000 souls, to be exact, 2000 promising souls of adventurers, souls with the most potential energy. They could have flourished if we hadn't betrayed them. But they will be stronger now that I have risen them in the name of my RAVEN QUEEN. Now they serve ME, I create demand for adventure, which causes towns to collect coin, which lures adventurers, which brings me POWER!\"\n112-\n113:**FINKLE IS A LICH.** This is a hopeless fight for the adventurers. Finkle raises his fingers and every kobold stops dead -- from within each kobold, a GHOUL emerges from the spine, shedding its form like taking off clothing.\n114-\n115-Inside the phylactery is a DEMI-LICH skull. Destroying the phylactery releases the DEMI-LICH but stops the ritual.\n116-\n--\n118-\n119-### Summary Points (from Chapter 3 Summary)\n120-\n121:- The Lich is revealed as MORDRED, who chose to possess FINKLE HOLLINGSWORTH\n122:- MORDRED needed 2000 souls to complete the Ritual of Rebirth\n123:- STALWART convinced kobolds to join the fight against MORDRED (speaking Draconic)\n124-- SPROCKET touched the DEMI-LICH through mage hand, was thrust into a deep trance\n125-- The party destroyed the phylactery, freeing the DEMI-LICH (SABINE)\n126:- The DEMI-LICH turned on MORDRED\n127-- STALWART completed the ritual and a bright light engulfed the room\n128-\n129----\n--\n146-\n147-DIANA discovers a letter from XERXES, Grand Inquisitor of the Artificer's Guild:\n148-\n149:\"Play his game for now, but know that there are those who fight to free you as you stand. I have restored as many of your party's minds as I could manage. We've put pressure on MORDRED, in hopes he would need to conduct this very ceremony. One of the awakened MUST win the soul tether at all costs! Without you, the Artificer's Guild will be eradicated!\"\n150-\n151:DIANA flashes the hexbag with the Artificer's Guild sigil. MORDRED's voice:\n152-\n153-\"This particular band of adventurers cost me centuries of work. My beloved SABINE RA was awakened outside her phylactery, she shall never be whole again.\"\n154-\n155:Upon the last contender standing, MORDRED descends and flicks a droplet from his phylactery onto the champion. Immediately a connection is felt with an ancient and boundless life force, bitter and filled with malice.\n156-\n157----\n158-\n--\n164-\n165-XERXES: \"It never gets old meeting new people. Yes I am Xerxes, 500 year old bag of plant parts. No I am no longer human, I performed a desperate act a little over 450 years ago. I am a being of beings. My individual parts are organisms of their own.\"\n166-\n167:**How he revived the team:** The arena AND the ritual room were infiltrated by spies well before the party arrived. They broke MORDRED's phylactery. The ritual was originally to resurrect SABINE -- but SABINE never died. She was tricked by RAMSES into accepting his curse and instead of subsisting on souls of others, she consumed her own, crystallizing within that chamber for an eternity.\n168-\n169-**The History of SABINE, XERXES, and RAMSES:**\n170-\n--\n176-\n177-When the party broke the phylactery, SABINE was reborn with free will. She sacrificed her being to revive the party -- depositing a portion of her life in each awakened adventurer as a crystalline heart.\n178-\n179:**Why he hates FINKLE:** \"Any adventurer who defiles an ancient prison should know better than to try and TAKE anything! He was RAMSES when we put him in there but 500 years of subsisting on the ambient lifeforce of the ZENITH drove him mad.\"\n180-\n181----\n182-\n--\n184-\n185-### Summary (from Summary.txt -- the key session recap)\n186-\n187:DIANA waved her hexbag around the arena and was ragdolled by MORDRED. The gladiator battle began. The awakened learned to tell friend from foe by the light in their eyes.\n188-\n189-DALLOR DALLORBILL was accused of being a turncoat and knocked unconscious by his own party. BROTOR ROOTBEARD stabilized him.\n190-\n191:The Artificer's Guild invaded through teleportation gates. XERXES arrived through 3 active gates. MORDRED declared TINY RICK as his heir, drenching him with his last phylactery contents. RICK's soul began to be infected.\n192-\n193-SPROCKET unlocked a message from SABINE planted in his psyche.\n194-\n195-The awakened escaped to the ARTIFICER'S GUILD mobile headquarters, which travels via portals (4 colors). XERXES told the full tale of RAMSES, SABINE, and the founding of the two colleges.\n196-\n197:The party must travel through the red door to the HALL OF THE RAVEN to create their own phylactery and complete the ritual to cleanse themselves from MORDRED's grasp.\n198-\n199-At the graveyard entrance, NYX lit a brazier causing undead to emerge. The party used 4 warding torches to fight them back. DALLOR DALLORBILL looted coins from zombie eyes.\n200-\n--\n216-\n217-- TINY RICK died -- losing 1/10th of a goddess avatar caused interdimensional portals\n218-- VARYS arrived from another world where there is no RAVEN QUEEN, only VECNA\n219:- V-MORDRED in the other world is the Avatar of VECNA\n220-- GUENIVIVE appeared in the other world with an Artificer's Guild hexbag\n221-- The more avatars fracture their souls, the weaker the boundary between worlds\n222-- The path of Light and Dark riddle solutions discovered through trial and error\n223:- Both teams got different halves of the riddle for cleansing MORDRED's curse\n224-\n225----\n226-\n--\n228-\n229-### DM's Prose\n230-\n231:FINKLE HOLLINGSWORTH appears, hooded:\n232-\n233:\"I'm so glad to see that you managed to survive! I had some unfinished business in this neck of the old forest. You saved my life from unending torment as MORDRED's vessel.\"\n234-\n235:FINKLE offers: passage to ARGOS, the SCARLET KEEP guild hall, promotion and 250gp for delivering THE HEART OF THE WILDE (a massive emerald from the MACHU people).\n236-\n237-They board the THORNBERRY SHADE under CAPTAIN LIZA. The crew includes: a fancy ranchero with a ten-gallon hat (ACE), 2 mafiosos (the DONs), a scientist gnome, 2 dwarves, a large elephant with handlers, and a little girl holding hands with a woman in a voodoo mask (SALLY and CERCI).\n238-\n--\n241-A murder mystery aboard the ship:\n242-- The weremoth is SALLY, a princess of MACHU\n243-- CERCI the witchdoctor created dreamcatchers that awaken the royal bloodline's shadow protector\n244:- Night 1: ACE steals the HEART OF THE WILDE (hidden in his ten-gallon hat), the guard is killed by the weremoth while in SALLY's room\n245-- Night 2: DUDLEY and VIKTORIA sneak to the kitchen, the weremoth kills DUDLEY and a DON\n246-- Night 3: The archaeologist figures out SALLY's identity and is killed while studying the weremoth\n247-- The dreamcatcher is the transformation catalyst -- it wards against threats near SALLY\n--\n285-\n286-SHEILA the Witch Hunter arrived on horseback, saving the day with radiant light. She revealed: RUTH was the greatest diviner in the realm, corrupted by the witching hat when SHEILA wore it first. RUTH sacrificed herself to take the hat.\n287-\n288:Discovery: running water removes witching hats. The missing children were being used to raise death knights -- clearly MORDRED is pulling strings.\n289-\n290:The party rainstormed RUTH's hat off, saved HANSEL and GRETTLE from a ritual involving a fiery eye of MORDRED over a bottomless pit. RUTH gave the prophecy. 4 tarot cards: Tiger (wild), Forge (creation), Holy Knight (faith), Family (progenitors).\n291-\n292----\n293-\n--\n312-\n313-The party entered via the owlbear cave with DON MONONUCHI's magmin summoning briefcase. They discovered:\n314-- Floor -1: Kindergarten watched by a night hag tormenting children's dreams\n315:- Floor -2: Holding cells for Artificers Guild prisoners (XERXES was overtaken by MORDRED's madness)\n316-- Floor -3: Turning chamber with 2 green hags and a cauldron (DON DAMIACHI turned into a dog)\n317:- Floor -4: Pentagram with eternal flame, soul-bag on altar as sacrifice to MORDRED\n318-\n319:The hags revealed: PESTILENCE is corrupt souls, brewed from kidnapped children's tainted dreams. MORDRED sent DANELLE and MARKUS as his agents. There is a bounty on the \"awakened.\"\n320-\n321----\n322-\n--\n330-\n331-Civil war in the courtyard. DOLF the blacksmith rallies defenders. The Revenant of JORGEN appears inside the Scarlet Keep.\n332-\n333:FINKLE confesses: \"I undermined the very institution I helped build. Sent countless to their deaths. I never found out what happened to those GOLDEN REACH recruits whose portraits hung in the keep.\"\n334-\n335-At MANDU's manor: MARKUS and DANELLE (in demon form) are interrogating a petrified orc woman -- MIA, THE EARTH MOTHER, one of the Argosian Woods-Witches. She was forced to wipe the adventurers' memories and compel them to join the STB.\n336-\n--\n423-\n424-### DM's Planning Notes (Chapter 20)\n425-\n426:DIERDRA CRESTFALLEN introduced: a 12th-level cleric of BAHAMUT, a \"risen\" who owes her life to the deity. THE HEART OF FAITH has been stolen by a great white dragon. A plague of the soul is spreading -- MORDRED is using PESTILENCE to destabilize areas, particularly the Winter's Curators who guard the Winter's Zenith.\n427-\n428----\n429-\n--\n674-- **The Artificer's Guild** -- Founded by SABINE to study animancy (life magic)\n675-- **The Circle of the Murder** -- Founded by SABINE to study necromancy (death magic)\n676-- **The Qual'Xar** -- Secret seafaring order bound by the MATURIN PACT\n677:- **The Golden Reach** -- DANELLE (succubus) and MARKUS (incubus), MORDRED's agents\n678-- **Bartrim's Brezerkers** -- Exiled adventurers from Vag'Mahar\n679-- **The Forgesworn** -- Bearers of the 13 fireforged artifacts\n680-- **The Shattered** -- DUSKEN WAYNE's faction of drider-converted drow\n--\n684-\n685-SABINE RA of ARGOS, avatar of the RAVEN QUEEN, founded two colleges to study the balance of life and death. She married XERXES and trusted her teacher RAMSES. RAMSES grew jealous, became a LICH, forced lichdom on SABINE. She starved herself and crystallized for 500 years.\n686-\n687:When the party broke her prison, SABINE sacrificed her being to give them crystalline hearts. MORDRED (the mad form of RAMSES) seeks to gather these hearts and forcibly resurrect SABINE to control her power.\n688-\n689:XERXES, a 500-year-old being of plant organisms, leads the Artificer's Guild against MORDRED.\n690-\n691-## GARD'DAEMON\n692-"}]},"uuid":"dd8ad2d6-405e-40ec-86ac-ee112cf13272","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:21.027Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c072126f-4956-4929-ae26-e03f6e46868c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"d46dd172-37f1-4f88-a905-d432c230165a","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a93464a70d5231c6f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01GekLAt224xirfsrxxvxDLH","type":"tool_result","content":"39-### 1.2 Jorgen's Role -- Rescuer or Trap-Layer?\n40-\n41-**DM Version (Chapter 0):**\n42:> Jorgen is \"a human ranger in a gold tabard\" who \"comes sprinting in pursued by flying kobolds.\" He is an established Brigade member (gold tunic = veteran). After the kobold fight, Finkle assigns the sewer mission.\n43-\n44-**Player Version (Summary 0-1):**\n45:> Jorgen \"claimed that the Kobolds had followed him across town from the sewers and had killed his previous set of recruits.\" Additionally, \"Dallor Dallorbill was able to swipe a note written to Jorgen from Finkle\" listing the recruits' names, classes, and arrival time -- implying Finkle and Jorgen are coordinating.\n46-\n47:The player version adds the stolen note detail, which is critical: Finkle knew exactly who was coming and when. The note makes Jorgen complicit from the beginning -- he is not a random ranger, he is bait.\n48-\n49-**Pilot Version:** Jorgen arrives at the tavern (Panel 43) bleeding, with shapes behind him. He leads the party into the sewers and betrays them in the ritual chamber (Panels 67-68).\n50-\n--\n52-\n53----\n54-\n55:### 1.3 Finkle's Introduction vs. the Pilot's Recruitment\n56-\n57-**DM Version (Chapter 0):**\n58:> Finkle is the Brigade's COO. He asks \"mysterious questions about ancient cults in the area, particularly the Raven Queen cult.\" He reveals that cult members \"use necromancy and animancy, view death as a steroid, and mark themselves with symbols under their eyelids.\"\n59-\n60-**Player Version (Summary 0-1):**\n61-> \"Broden Rootbeard received an inspiration event point and the following information about the Raven Queen Cult\" -- confirming one player was particularly engaged with this lore drop.\n--\n63-**Brigit's Chapter 5 Notes:**\n64-> \"He's why we don't remember signing up for the brigade\" -- Brigit's notes reveal that Mordred manipulated the party into joining. They did not volunteer.\n65-\n66:**Pilot Version:** Finkle is a \"gnome bureaucrat/drill sergeant\" (Panels 35-38) running a recruitment yard. The Raven Queen cult questions are not present. The pilot treats recruitment as straightforward military onboarding.\n67-\n68:**CANON FOR PILOT**: The pilot's streamlined Finkle is correct for pacing. However, the \"not remembering signing up\" detail from Brigit is enormously important for later episodes. Plant it subtly: in Panel 34 (recruits waiting), one character could have a confused expression or whisper \"Did you sign up for this?\" to another. This becomes a thread for Episode 2.\n69-\n70----\n71-\n--\n94-> The ritual chamber has \"a massive open chamber with a steeped roof and four endless waterfalls of orange, red, blue and green liquid/pseudo-liquid into a grate below. In the center of the room on top of a massive gear-like pedestal sits a Phylactery of the Raven Queen. Under the pedestal is grooved with tracks from the center to each of the 4 streams. Additionally, there are mysterious markings on the tracks. Along the walls to the room are a total of 12 tunnels to Kobold nests.\"\n95-\n96-**Player Version (Summary 2):**\n97:> Finkle is revealed as \"actually a Lich named Mordred.\" The phylactery \"contains a Demi-Lich of current unknown origin\" (later revealed as Sabine). The four liquids are more precisely described: \"RED blood, hot to the touch; ORANGE mercury mixed with molten iron; BLUE liquid souls; GREEN poison, corrosive like acid.\"\n98-\n99-**Pilot Version:** The ritual chamber (Panel 64) has four waterfalls but recasts them as: blood-red (sacrifice), acid-green (pestilence), mercury-orange (war), soul-blue (death). No phylactery is visible in the pilot. The altar bears the Raven Queen's crest. The sealed steel door with concentric rings is the focus (Panels 71-74).\n100-\n--\n105-### 1.6 The Control Room and Half-Ogres\n106-\n107-**DM Version (Chapter 0):**\n108:> Half the party \"find Jorgen and Finkle's Half Ogre henchmen Stromble and Grom or Gromble and Strom in a control room pulling levers (priming the ritual chamber).\"\n109-\n110-**Player Version (Summary 0-1):**\n111-> Same detail, but adds: \"They are caught off guard by the sudden arrival and struggle to explain why they are here (clearly lying).\"\n--\n113-**Brigit's Chapter 5 Notes:**\n114-> References \"Stromble and Grom\" from earlier sessions, confirming their presence.\n115-\n116:**Pilot Version:** The half-ogres are present as Finkle's bodyguards in the recruitment yard (Panels 35, 38) but do NOT appear in the underground sequence. The control room is absent.\n117-\n118-**CANON FOR PILOT**: The pilot is correct to exclude the control room. The underground sequence in the pilot is about atmosphere and discovery, not mechanical puzzle-solving. The half-ogres serve better as foreshadowing in the yard -- their presence hints at \"why does a gnome bureaucrat need two half-ogre bodyguards?\"\n119-\n--\n134-\n135----\n136-\n137:### 1.8 The Betrayal -- Finkle/Mordred vs. Jorgen\n138-\n139-**DM Version (Chapter 2):**\n140:> The betrayer is *Finkle*, who is revealed as Mordred the Lich. Jorgen is his mercenary/kobold trainer, ultimately disposable. Mordred commands Stalwart to kill Jorgen because \"he had outlived his usefulness.\"\n141-\n142-**Player Version (Summary 2):**\n143:> Same. Finkle = Mordred. Jorgen is a tool. The real threat was the gnome all along.\n144-\n145-**Pilot Version:**\n146:> Jorgen is the betrayer (Panels 67-68). No Mordred reveal. No Finkle-is-a-lich twist. The pilot ends on the sealed door, not the ritual completion.\n147-\n148:**CANON FOR PILOT**: The pilot is correct. The Mordred/Finkle reveal is the Episode 2 cliffhanger. The pilot's Jorgen betrayal is a *different* kind of twist -- not \"the friendly gnome is actually the big bad\" but \"the competent rescuer led you here on purpose.\" This works better for a first episode because it teaches the audience rule #1 of Mardov: trust is earned, not granted by role.\n149-\n150----\n151-\n--\n361-| 32 | Bird's-eye view: eight figures converging. | Fate assembling its pieces. | None | Wide, almost abstract. All roads lead to one yard. |\n362-| 33 | Recruitment yard exterior. Scarlet banners. | Military bureaucracy with teeth. | None | Per DM Ch 0: the Scarlet Tunic Brigade is a private military company, not a government force. The banners should look corporate, not royal. |\n363-| 34 | Recruits waiting in the yard. Uncomfortable clusters. | Strangers sizing each other up. | Player authority: someone should look confused about being there. Per Brigit Ch 5: \"He's why we don't remember signing up.\" A subtle \"wait, when did I sign up for this?\" reaction. | Diverse group. Tension. Nobody trusts anyone. |\n364:| 35 | Finkle's entrance. Door slam. Tiny gnome, huge guards. | Comedy of scale. Absolute authority in a tiny package. | None | Per DM: \"bookish gnome flanked by two half-ogre henchmen.\" Per pilot: Finkle stands on a crate. Immaculate suit that does not fit. |\n365:| 36 | Finkle commands from his crate. | Pure disdain. | Per DM: Finkle asks about Raven Queen cults here. For pilot: streamline to military briefing. | Low angle shot. Pointing. The crate gives him height he does not naturally possess. |\n366:| 37 | The triangle management diagram. | Dark comedy. | FINKLE (top) / EVERYONE ELSE (bottom). This is pure DM comedy that the pilot preserves perfectly. | Chalk on slate or parchment. |\n367:| 38 | Finkle introduces his bodyguards with dismissive gesture. | \"These are mine. You are not.\" | Per player notes: the half-ogres are Stromble and Grom. Their names are a running joke (Gromble/Stromble, Strom/Grom). For the pilot, they are silent and enormous. | Two mountains of flesh flanking a pebble of a gnome. |\n368-| 39 | Sparring in the yard. Contrasting styles. | Raw potential, unrefined. | None | Wooden weapons. Two recruits testing each other. |\n369-| 40 | Dismissed. Recruits disperse in small groups. | The party has not formed yet. These are still strangers. | None | Long shadows. Evening approaching. |\n370-| 41 | Tavern exterior. Warm light, wooden sign. | The last moment of normalcy. | None | Wet cobblestones. Per Summary 8: \"the Galloping Galoshes\" is the lower-ward tavern. Use a similar warm, shabby aesthetic. |\n--\n379-| Panel | HAPPENS | FEELS | SAID | LOOKS |\n380-|-------|---------|-------|------|-------|\n381-| 45 | Tavern aftermath. Dead kobolds. Wrecked interior. | Breath. Assessment. \"What just happened?\" | Per player notes: Dallor should be pocketing something from a kobold corpse. | Debris, dripping ale, heavy breathing. Per pilot: \"injuries matter.\" |\n382:| 46 | A note is produced. Fussy gnome handwriting. | The breadcrumb that leads underground. | Per DM Ch 0: Finkle assigns the sewer mission. Per player version: Dallor stole a note from Jorgen listing recruits' names and arrival time. BOTH notes should exist. The mission note is official. The stolen note (seen briefly, not read) is the hint. | Folded parchment. Small precise handwriting. |\n383-| 47 | Party huddle around a table. | The decision to descend. | None. But the body language should say: reluctance, curiosity, duty, greed (one face per motivation). | Uplit faces. The last warm light. |\n384-| 48 | Jorgen at the bar, briefing the party. | He knows more than he is saying. | Per player notes: Jorgen frames this as pest control. \"Collect four sources of ancient magic to disperse the kobold infestation.\" He makes it sound routine. | Competent, professional, lying. |\n385-| 49 | Sewer entrance. Iron grate, stairs into darkness. | Crossing the threshold. No return. | Per player Summary 0-1: \"Jorgen pulled a lever... Immediately upon entry the door slammed shut and the sewer entrance was sealed.\" Add a SOUND CUE of stone grinding shut behind them. | Descending into darkness. The warm world vanishes. |\n--\n407-[Omitted long context line]\n408-| 65 | Sprocket's close-up reaction. | Wonder beyond calculation. His analytical mind is overwhelmed. | Per player Summary 2: Sprocket's response to the phylactery was to reach out with mage hand -- \"As soon as the magic connection was made with the Demi-Lich, Sprocket was thrust into a deep trance.\" His instinct is always to TOUCH, to UNDERSTAND. Here, he is stopped by awe. | Tan skin, golden hair, dark eyes. Lit from below by four colors. Goggles on forehead. |\n409-| 66 | Close-up: blood-red waterfall. | Visceral wrongness. This is not water. | Per player Summary 0-1: \"RED blood, hot to the touch.\" Per Summary 2: \"The liquid mixed with the bubbling semi-clear liquid that was already occupying the container, however the massive vial never overflowed despite the endless waterfall.\" The liquid has properties that defy physics. | Thick, luminous, steaming. Almost-shapes within. |\n410:| 67 | Jorgen's mask drops. | Betrayal revealed. | Per player Summary 2: Finkle/Mordred \"command[ed] Stalwart Little to kill Jorgon because he had outlived his usefulness as a mercenary/kobold trainer.\" Jorgen is a tool, not the mastermind. But in the pilot, his betrayal is the twist. The audience does not yet know Mordred exists. | Four-waterfall light. Cold, purposeful expression. No pretense. |\n411-| 68 | Jorgen draws his weapon against the party. | Shock. Violence. Trust destroyed. | None | Per pilot: \"Four-colored light casts his shadow in four directions simultaneously.\" Four shadows = four horsemen. A visual plant for the Zenith system. |\n412-| 69 | Combat: Sprocket at the gear mechanism. | Desperate resourcefulness. The gnome's instinct: understand, then act. | Per player Summary 2: Sprocket \"dealt the first blow\" to the phylactery in the original fight. His hands are always working. | Green cape, earth tones, small hands on massive machinery. |\n413-| 70 | Chamber post-combat. Waterfalls unchanged. | Cosmic indifference. Human violence is irrelevant to what this place is. | None | Per player Summary 2: after the ritual fight, the room continued functioning. The waterfalls poured. The magic did not care who won. The pilot captures this: \"The waterfalls do not care.\" |\n--\n439-2. **How it FEELS**: Player authority. Brigit's real-time notes capture emotional reality the DM summaries cannot.\n440-3. **What is SAID**: Player quotes > DM paraphrases. The DM summarizes; the players remember exact words and running gags.\n441-4. **How it LOOKS**: Merge both. The DM provides architectural detail (four waterfalls, gear-pedestal, 12 kobold tunnels). The players provide atmospheric detail (the Sistine-chapel ceiling, the eerie chanting, the Scooby-Doo vibe).\n442:5. **Pilot adaptation overrides both** when it makes a deliberate creative choice (relocating to Conquis Abbey, \"KNEEL\" instead of \"RUN LEFT,\" Jorgen as the primary betrayer instead of Finkle/Mordred).\n443-\n444----\n445-"}]},"uuid":"109dc705-7ed4-4f57-aa8c-fe65e1c710f0","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:22.378Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"d46dd172-37f1-4f88-a905-d432c230165a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"50adab7d-ff1f-4c0d-a25b-2d0ff6a8e886","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"a93464a70d5231c6f","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_014CUah2D6JWwSCH8K1VcJ8S","type":"tool_result","content":"48-|   |   |\n49-|   |   |-- OPERATIONS:\n50-|   |   |   |-- Scarlet Tunic Brigade (soul-harvesting front -- 2,000 souls)\n51:|   |   |   |-- Possessed Finkle Hollingsworth as vessel/disguise\n52-|   |   |   |-- Deployed Jorgen as lure to recruit adventurers\n53-|   |   |   |-- Golden Reach (Markus/Danelle, incubus/succubus agents in Argos)\n54-|   |   |   |-- Hag Coven (Margaret, Gertrude, Tabitha -- brew Pestilence\n--\n338-- Immediately trusted by the new recruits\n339-\n340-**The Evidence (Chapter 1):**\n341:- Dallor pickpockets a note from Jorgen showing Finkle knew the recruits' arrival time glamour (Chapter 19)\n354-- Even as a soul powering Nyx's disguise, Jorgen's vengeful voice echoes: \"KILL THE GNOME\"\n--\n362-\n363-| Chapter | Double-Cross / Reveal | Who Was Fooled |\n364-|---------|----------------------|----------------|\n365:| 0-2 | Finkle Hollingsworth is Mordred; Scarlet Tunic Brigade is a soul-harvesting operation | The party, 2,000 adventurers |\n366-| 1 souls for the Winter's Zenith |\n--\n499-\n500-### Moments Where Brigitte's Notes Show Players Figured Something Out Early\n501-\n502:- **Dallor's pickpocket (Ch. 1):** Players caught the Jorgen/Finkle setup before the DM's reveal, via Dallor stealing a note\n503-- **Stalwart's divine sense (Ch. 11):** Immediately exposed the Golden Reach as fiends at the Festival of Light\n504-- **Brigitte's zodiac connection:** Players began connecting zodiac lore to their character arcs before the DM fully revealed the system\n505-- **\"What the fuck is going on?\" (Ch. 100):** Brigitte's chapter title -- the Shadowfell/time dilation arc genuinely baffled players, showing DM succeeded in maintaining mystery even 100 chapters deep\n--\n508-### Red Herrings That Worked\n509-\n510-- **The Scarlet Tunic Brigade's innocence:** Players genuinely believed they were joining a legitimate adventuring company\n511:- **Finkle as a friendly NPC:** The gnome COO persona was so convincing that even after the reveal, the party eventually accepted the REAL Finkle back as an ally\n512-- **The Sunless Citadel as Winter's Zenith:** Stalwart expected to find the Zenith there; instead found the Shield of Retribution. The real Zenith was inside Mt. Bynar\n513-- **Reynaldine as a pure villain:** His Vecna-servant persona lasted the majority of the campaign before his true motivation (saving Gabriel) was revealed\n514-\n--\n636-|\n637-+-- KEY ALLIANCES:\n638-    +-- Artificer's Guild (Xerxes, compromised)\n639:    +-- Finkle Hollingsworth (freed from Mordred's possession)\n640-    +-- Captain Liza / Thornberry Shade crew / Qual'Xar\n641-    +-- Bartrim's Brezerkers (Forgesworn protectors)\n642-    +-- Tiberious D. Rex (Bahamut cleric, messenger)\n--\n709-\n710-### Threads Planted in Pilot Scope\n711-\n712:1. **Finkle/Mordred's Disguise** -- The entire pilot operates under the deception that this is a standard adventuring company. Every friendly interaction with Finkle is dramatic irony.\n713-\n714-2. **Jorgen's Complicity** -- His arrival (sprinting from kobolds, gold tabard, lost his previous party) must read as heroic on first pass and sinister on rewatch. The gold tabard is Mordred's color. \"Lost his previous party\" means he led them to death.\n715-\n--\n717-\n718-4. **Sabine Ra's Sacrifice** -- The Demi-Lich's choice to die so the party can live is the emotional core. The crystalline hearts deposited in each member are the campaign's MacGuffin.\n719-\n720:5. **Dallor's Pickpocket** -- The note revealing Finkle knew the recruits' arrival time. This is the evidence thread -- the moment the audience (through Dallor) learns the truth before the full reveal.\n721-\n722-6. **The 2,000 Souls** -- Mordred's line about harvesting 2,000 adventurer souls establishes the scale of evil. The undead audience in the colosseum (Chapter 3) visualizes this.\n723-\n--\n728-- Four-colored motifs (red, green, blue, orange) appearing subtly -- matching the four Zeniths\n729-- A blood star barely visible in the sky (the recurring omen)\n730-- Kobold eyes with the unnatural blue glow of Mordred's undead magic\n731:- Finkle's shadow slightly wrong -- too tall, too gaunt, not matching his gnome body\n732-\n733-**Dialogue seeds:**\n734:- Finkle asking about \"ancient cults\" and the Raven Queen cult -- seemingly academic, actually reconnaissance\n735:- Finkle noting that cult members \"mark themselves with symbols under their eyelids\" -- he knows because he IS one\n736-- \"SCARLET, SCARLET, SCARLET\" chanted by kobolds -- they know the Brigade's true nature\n737-\n738-**Thematic seeds:**\n--\n760-- Any of the four Horsemen\n761-- The prophecies\n762-\n763:The pilot's job is to make the audience feel safe, then rip that safety away. In the study, [[Sprocket]] uses a vision from [[Sabine Ra]] to find and shatter [[Mordred]]'s original phylactery. A voice identified as Ramses begs them to take his crystal to the [[Forest's Zenith]] so he can finally die. The four liquids are combined at the altar in the ritual order (red, orange, blue, green), and with a drop of blood from each afflicted member, warmth returns -- the cleansing is complete.\n20-\n21:Outside, the party meets a hooded [[Finkle Hollingsworth]], now freed from [[Mordred]]'s possession. He offers passage to [[argos]] aboard [[Captain Liza]]'s riverboat the [[Thornberry Shade]], promises them the [[Scarlet Keep]] as a base of operations, and 250gp each for delivering the [[Heart of the Wilde]] (a massive emerald he stole from the [[Forest's Zenith]]).\n22-\n23:[Omitted long matching line]\n24-\n25-## Key Events\n26-\n27-- Cleansing ritual completed; party freed from [[Mordred]]'s curse\n28-- Ramses's crystal recovered; begs to be taken to [[Forest's Zenith]]\n29:- [[Finkle Hollingsworth|Finkle]] returns as ally; offers [[Scarlet Keep]] and [[Heart of the Wilde]] delivery job\n30-- Boarding the [[Thornberry Shade]] with [[Captain Liza]]\n31-- [[Jorgen]]'s revenant appears chanting \"Kill the gnome\"\n32:- Murder mystery: Gordon killed, [[Heart of the Wilde]] stolen\n33-- WereMoth revealed as [[sally]]'s royal protector\n34-- Ace/Chuck Wheeler exposed as gem thief\n35-- Dons exposed as kidnappers hired by Jafar ([[sally]]'s uncle)\n--\n37-## Character Moments\n38-\n39-- [[Sprocket]] channels [[Sabine Ra]]'s vision to shatter the phylactery\n40:- [[Finkle Hollingsworth]] returns humbled and seeking redemption\n41-- The party plays detective aboard {"parentUuid":"20591d1e-32e4-4e62-b8b5-09c0bbcd33f1","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a93464a70d5231c6f","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01EaMisM2Hh3QWm9VVBgnhMo","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"I now have comprehensive data. Here is the complete timeline:\n\n---\n\n# Finkle Hollingsworth -- Complete Campaign Timeline\n\n## CRITICAL NOTE: Finkle Hollingsworth is a **GNOME**, not a goblin.\n\n---\n\n## Pre-Campaign\n\n### Mordred Possesses Finkle at Forest's Zenith\n- **[CANON]** Mordred (also known as Ramses, Sabine Ra's former teacher turned lich) possesses the gnome Finkle Hollingsworth at the Forest's Zenith. The timeline places this in the \"Recent Past (decades before campaign)\" era, alongside other pre-campaign events like the Ned'Zailok siege of Tower Bridge.\n  - Source: `lore/timeline.md` line 26: \"Mordred possesses Finkle Hollingsworth (Gnome, NOT Goblin) at Forest's Zenith\"\n  - Source: `canon-reconciliation.md` line 779: \"Mordred possesses Finkle Hollingsworth at Forest's Zenith\"\n\n### Mordred Steals the Heart of the Wilde\n- **[CANON]** While at the Forest's Zenith, Mordred (in Finkle's body) steals the Heart of the Wilde, a massive emerald keystone. This is the first Zenith to be compromised.\n  - Source: `story-arc-dm-notes.md` line 97: \"The Forest's Zenith has already been partially plundered by Mordred (he had the Heart of the Wilde and used it)\"\n  - Source: `dm-notes/dnd overarching story.md` line 35: \"first zenith has already been plundered by MORDRED, he already had the heart of the wild and used it\"\n\n### Founding the Scarlet Tunic Brigade\n- **[CANON]** Mordred founds the Scarlet Tunic Brigade as a soul-harvesting front, using Finkle's gnome bureaucrat persona as the COO. The Brigade provides legitimate military services (including covering the Tower Bridge evacuation against Ned'Zailok) while secretly luring adventurers to ritual chambers to harvest their souls.\n  - Source: `lore/timeline.md` line 27: \"Scarlet Tunic Brigade founded as a soul-harvesting front\"\n  - Source: `factions/scarlet-tunic-brigade.md` lines 16-18\n\n### [INFERRED] Finkle's Family at the Forest's Zenith\n- **[CANON]** Finkle had a son, Archibald Hollingsworth. Archibald later reveals (Ch. 96) that when the Forest's Zenith opened, his mother touched the Heart of the Wilde and went mad (Crown of Madness). They were stuck in the Zenith until Finkle arrived with Machu elves. Finkle grabbed the gem; Jafar took on the Crown of Madness. Archibald and his mother died; Finkle recovered their bodies.\n  - **[INFERRED]** This event is likely when/how Mordred found and possessed Finkle -- the gnome went to the Zenith for legitimate reasons (his family was there), and Mordred seized the opportunity to use his body as a vessel, steal the Heart of the Wilde, and use Finkle's existing institutional connections to build the STB.\n  - Source: `ch096-brigit.md` lines 132, 240-248\n\n### ~500 Years of Soul Harvesting\n- **[CANON]** Under Mordred's control, using Finkle's identity, the STB harvests approximately 2,000 adventurer souls over an extended period. Jorgen serves as the lure, leading adventuring parties to ritual chambers beneath cities.\n  - Source: Chapter 2 dialogue: \"2000 souls, to be exact... They could have flourished if we hadn't betrayed them.\"\n\n### [INFERRED] The Real Finkle During Possession\n- **[INFERRED]** Xerxes's dialogue suggests Finkle was aware during Mordred's possession but unable to resist: \"Any adventurer who defiles an ancient prison should know better than to try and TAKE anything! He was RAMSES when we put him in there but 500 years of subsisting on the ambient lifeforce of the ZENITH drove him mad.\"\n  - Source: `dm-full-narrative-act1-2.md` line 179\n\n---\n\n## Campaign Era\n\n### Chapter 0: The Scarlet Tunic Brigade\n- **[CANON]** Finkle (still possessed by Mordred) appears as the Brigade's COO in Argos, flanked by half-ogre henchmen Stromble and Grom. He delivers the recruitment orientation, asking probing questions about the Raven Queen cult and ancient cults -- reconnaissance disguised as briefing. He assigns the party to descend into the sewers.\n  - Source: `chapters/act-1/Chapter 00 - The Scarlet Tunic Brigade.md`\n  - Key detail: His shadow was \"slightly wrong -- too tall, too gaunt, not matching his gnome body\" (from pilot adaptation notes in `canon-reconciliation.md`)\n\n### Chapter 1: The Setup Exposed\n- **[CANON]** Dallor Dallorbill pickpockets a note from Jorgen revealing that Finkle knew the precise arrival time, names, and classes of every recruit -- proof the recruitment was a trap.\n  - Source: `dm-full-narrative-act1-2.md` line 86\n\n### Chapter 2: \"The Gnome Eff'n Way\" -- Mordred Revealed\n- **[CANON]** Finkle's body contorts into a \"hollow, twisted, gaunt figure.\" He is revealed as Mordred, an ancient lich. He orders Jorgen killed for outliving his usefulness. The party shatters the Demi-Lich phylactery containing Sabine Ra. Sabine turns Mordred's own ghouls against him and forces him to flee. The party dies but is resurrected by Sabine's sacrifice, each receiving a crystalline heart fragment.\n  - Source: `chapters/act-1/Chapter 02 - The Gnome Effn Way.md`\n  - Quote: \"2000 souls, to be exact... They could have flourished if we hadn't betrayed them. But they will be stronger now that I have risen them in the name of my RAVEN QUEEN.\"\n\n### Chapters 3-6: Aftermath and Cleansing\n- **[CANON]** The party learns from Xerxes that Mordred was originally Ramses, Sabine's teacher. They enter the Hall of the Raven to cleanse themselves of Mordred's curse. Ramses's crystal begs them to take it to the Forest's Zenith so he can finally die.\n  - Source: `dm-full-narrative-act1-2.md` lines 167-197\n\n### Chapter 7: Finkle Freed, Heart of the Wilde Introduced\n- **[CANON]** The real Finkle Hollingsworth appears, hooded, now freed from Mordred's possession. He offers the party: passage to Argos, the Scarlet Keep as a guild hall, promotion, and 250gp each for delivering the Heart of the Wilde (the massive emerald he stole from the Forest's Zenith -- now in his possession post-liberation). Jorgen's revenant appears aboard the Thornberry Shade, chanting \"KILL THE GNOME.\"\n  - Source: `chapters/act-1/Chapter 07 - Night of the WereMoth.md` lines 21, 29-31\n  - Key detail: The Heart of the Wilde is aboard the ship, attracting a giant octopus. Ace/Chuck Wheeler steals the Heart (hiding it in his ten-gallon hat). A murder mystery unfolds around the theft.\n\n### Chapters 7-8: Jorgen's Revenant Pursues Finkle\n- **[CANON]** Finkle reappears and the Jorgen revenant attacks him, paralyzing him. Bergs hits the revenant with lightning. The revenant is eventually knocked overboard. Finkle disappears.\n  - Source: `ch007-brigit.md` lines 18, 64, 84\n\n### Chapter 8: Heart of the Wilde Secured\n- **[CANON]** The Thornberry Shade arrives in Argos. The Heart of the Wilde is recovered after the murder mystery/theft aboard ship.\n  - Source: `chapters/act-1/Chapter 08 - Arrival in Argos.md`\n\n### Chapter 13: Finkle's Confession\n- **[CANON]** During the civil war/liberation of Argos, Finkle confesses to the party: \"I undermined the very institution I helped build. Sent countless to their deaths. I never found out what happened to those GOLDEN REACH recruits whose portraits hung in the keep.\"\n  - Source: `dm-full-narrative-act1-2.md` line 333\n\n### [INFERRED] Chapters 14-81: Finkle Between Appearances\n- **[INFERRED]** After Chapter 13, Finkle largely disappears from the narrative. The Heart of the Wilde is placed in the Scarlet Keep but is stolen shortly after the party leaves.\n  - Source: `dm-notes/Story Beats To Hit.md` line 127: \"The Heart was placed in The Scarlet Keep until it was stolen shortly after Brigit and the others left\"\n  - **[SPECULATIVE]** During this period, Mordred's lingering influence -- or Vecna's direct manipulation -- begins reasserting control over Finkle. The DM notes reveal that \"Finkle was always going to be Vecna's body\" (ch096-brigit.md line 232), suggesting the gnome was a predetermined vessel. Finkle likely returned to the Forest's Zenith area (Machu/Eavallan region) where the Zenith's ambient necromantic energy accelerated his corruption.\n\n### Chapter 82T: Brigit Finds Finkle in Eavallan Forest\n- **[CANON]** After becoming a Progenitor through a ritual in Eden, Brigit exits into the Eavallan Forest. She hears a loud cackle: \"I DID IT.\" It is Finkle Hollingsworth. He is emaciated and decrepit -- \"something is definitely sucking its life force.\" He has the Heart of the Wilde, embedded as \"a giant green gem\" behind his left eye. He has been looking for Brigit because she has the Anchor of Death (the locket). He claims to be working with Leon. He seems to be talking to someone who isn't there. He is clearly being controlled by something else (\"it almost seems like something else is controlling him\"). Brigit breaks free of a dreamcatcher trap, turns into a Fire Elemental, and fights him. Finkle casts Energy Drain, tries to curse her. He pulls back his left eye revealing the Heart of the Wilde and tries to use it against her. He Tree Strides through a tree to escape. Brigit follows, landing in the Temple of Life and Death beneath Argos.\n  - Source: `ch082T-brigit.md` lines 332-498\n\n### Chapter 83T: Pursuit Through the Temple of Life and Death\n- **[CANON]** Brigit follows Finkle into the underground Temple of Life and Death beneath Argos. There is tons of pestilence causing madness. Finkle heads toward a large carved door, knocks in a pattern, slides in. Brigit hides and overhears: \"Finkle thinks she's here, lock the door.\" The walls are covered with dragon marks. Brigit reports to the party: \"Semi-safe. In death dungeon below argos, found crazy finkle. Gonna try to get more info and sneak out.\"\n  - Source: `ch083T-brigit.md` lines 30-120\n\n### [INFERRED] Chapters 84T-89: Finkle Consolidating at the Temple\n- **[INFERRED]** Finkle remains in/around the Temple of Life and Death beneath Argos. The party discusses plans to deal with him.\n  - Source: `ch090-brigit.md` line 76: \"What to do about finkle and how we get the heart of the wilde back.\"\n\n### Chapter 90: Temple of Life and Death Reconnaissance\n- **[CANON]** Brigit explores Machu and the House of Life and Death. The Forest's Zenith hangs above Machu. Runes wash the Zenith in gold. The party learns: Quelil/Breeze (a Child of the Forest in kenku form) is with Finkle, and \"some kind of Pestilence is being created out of them.\" Breeze declares she has come to \"reclaim the forest zenith.\"\n  - Source: `ch090-brigit.md` lines 222-267\n\n### Chapter 95T: The Party Plans to Kill Finkle\n- **[CANON]** The party decides: \"Let's go kill Finkle!\" They scry on him. He appears making a tar zenith-shape with his hands. As he twirls it, Quelil/Breeze loses her hue/soul. \"Finkle is combining with the heart of the wild, and he notices that I'm looking at him.\" He has an amalgamation of a previous Artificer's Guild member as a puppet, controlled via magic mouth. The party engages in combat. \"Pursuit and Protect move to go kill Finkle.\"\n  - Source: `ch095T-brigit.md` lines 58-246\n\n### Chapter 96: \"Non-Finkle\" Dies, Revelations\n- **[CANON]** The party fights \"not-Finkle\" / \"non-Finkle\" -- a construct/puppet form. Nocturnus moves up and engages the figure. The party attacks with Bigsby's Hand, magic missiles, fire. Non-Finkle's mechanical suit breaks down. He attempts to plane shift but is bitten by Nocturnus. \"Non-finkle is dead -- two marks on the side of his neck. Pouring down his mouth is black blood.\"\n  - Key revelation: The gnome they meet afterward is **Archibald Hollingsworth -- Finkle's son**. Reynardine explains: \"Finkle was turning into a lich, and with all of the pieces, he became Ramses/Vecna.\" \"With the heart of the wild, he owns the forest of death and is probably now coming for Brigit who owns the anchor of death, the locket.\" \"Finkle was always going to be Vecna's body.\"\n  - Source: `ch096-brigit.md` lines 30-80, 132-248\n\n### [INFERRED] Chapters 97-134: Finkle/Mordred at Forest's Zenith\n- **[INFERRED]** After the \"non-Finkle\" puppet is destroyed in Chapter 96, the real Finkle (now fully merged with Mordred/Ramses and serving as Vecna's vessel) retreats to the Forest's Zenith above Machu. He uses the Heart of the Wilde to awaken the Forest Guardian (the dungeon boss of the Forest's Zenith). He establishes the demi-lich phylactery in the Zenith's anchor room. His body continues deteriorating into undeath.\n  - Source: `story-arc-dm-notes.md` line 97-99: \"Mordred had the Heart of the Wilde and used it to awaken the forest guardian -- the big boss... After defeating the forest guardian, the Heart of the Wilde transports the ENTIRE party to V-MARDOV\"\n  - Source: `vecna-sworn.md` line 39: \"Forest Guardian / Forest Titan -- awakened by Mordred using the Heart of the Wilde\"\n  - **[SPECULATIVE]** During this period, Finkle-Mordred is completing the preparation of the Forest's Zenith for Gard'Daemon. He is the Vecna Sworn's primary agent at the Death Zenith, using the Heart of the Wilde to control the Forest and the Anchor of Death's chamber. His body is no longer meaningfully \"Finkle\" -- it is Mordred's lich-form wearing Finkle like a husk, now further transformed into Vecna's vessel.\n\n### Chapter 131: Party Acknowledges the Problem\n- **[CANON]** The party takes stock: \"We are missing the heart of the wilde, Brigit has Anchor of Death, no one can control the Forest Zenith.\"\n  - Source: `player-narrative-act3-4.md` line 2275\n\n### Chapter 133: Forest's Zenith Confirmed Open\n- **[CANON]** The Forest's Zenith was already opened. \"Finkle took the heart of the wilde.\" This is stated as established fact during the party's strategic planning.\n  - Source: `player-narrative-act3-4.md` line 620\n\n### Chapter 135: Zombie Finkle and the Demi-Lich Phylactery\n- **[CANON]** Brigit scries on the anchor room of the Forest's Zenith. She sees: \"There's a familiar sight in the cylinder, and it killed Brigit. This is the demi-lich phylactery of the Raven Queen.\" And: \"Finkle is also in the room, and is walking in a circle, very zombie shambling.\" The Zenith's anchor room contains an acrid spiraling waterspout/glass cylinder holding the demi-lich phylactery, and Finkle is now a zombie -- mindlessly circling as a guardian or remnant.\n  - Source: `ch136-brigit.md` (scrying scene) lines 272-282\n  - Source: `character-timelines.md` line 76: \"Scries on Forest Zenith anchor room: sees demi-lich phylactery and zombie Finkle\"\n\n### Chapter 136: Ash Falls, Forest Zenith Activated\n- **[CANON]** Ash falls from the sky at Var'tos, meaning the Forest's Zenith has been activated. Brigit's locket is pulsating with acrid green (usually emerald). Her elemental powers are gone. When Brigit speaks with a dead black dragon, she learns: \"Vecna is on Mardov\" and wants the Death Zenith. Notably: \"not Finkle\" -- Vecna himself, not an avatar or a lich, is present.\n  - Source: `ch136-brigit.md` lines 26, 234\n  - Source: `character-timelines.md` lines 90-93: \"Must find Forest Zenith and confront zombie Finkle + demi-lich phylactery\"\n\n---\n\n## Unresolved Threads (as of Chapter 136)\n\n- **[CANON]** Brigit must confront the Forest's Zenith: zombie Finkle + demi-lich phylactery remain in the anchor room\n- **[CANON]** The Heart of the Wilde is unrecovered -- no one controls the Forest's Zenith\n- **[CANON]** Brigit has the Anchor of Death but has lost her elemental powers\n- **[CANON]** Varris is designated to sit in the Forest's Zenith control room during the final battle (Gard'Daemon)\n- **[CANON]** The DM's plan: defeating the Forest Guardian (awakened by Mordred using the Heart of the Wilde) transports the entire party to V-Mardov for the V-Mordred confrontation\n- **[CANON]** The \"star-crossed three\" (Sabine, Ramses/Mordred, Xerxes) must be brought to peace at the Forest's Zenith for resolution\n\n---\n\n## Summary of Finkle's Arc\n\nFinkle Hollingsworth is a gnome bureaucrat whose body was stolen by the lich Mordred at the Forest's Zenith. He was used as a puppet to run the Scarlet Tunic Brigade's soul-harvesting operation for centuries. After being freed in Chapter 7, he briefly served as an ally and confessed his guilt. But he was re-corrupted -- the Heart of the Wilde fused into his eye, and he became progressively more lich-like. By Chapter 96, Reynardine confirms Finkle \"was turning into a lich, and with all of the pieces, he became Ramses/Vecna\" and \"was always going to be Vecna's body.\" By Chapter 135, Finkle is a
